The two are orthogonal — Yandex Maps declares the former (`resizeable=true`) but explicitly sets `resizeableActivity="false"` on its main activity, which is the actual blocker. Fixed: grep is now `'resizeMode\|resizeableActivity\|maxAspectRatio\|launchMode\|targetSdk'` — `resizeMode` and `resizeableActivity` are the AOSP `pm dump` keys for the activity-level flag. **New helper.** Added `shellSync(Context, String) → String` (60 LoC private static), used by `probeDisplays` and `probeClusterDisplayGeometry` to run sub-probes synchronously (CountDownLatch via Object.wait/notify, 6 s timeout) and merge their stdout into the calling test's `r.detail`. Reuses the same `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` plumbing as `shellCapture`. **Catalog wording.** All R13–R18 + R23 + R26–R28 descriptions in `catalog()` were rewritten in English, with an inline note explaining the AOSP verb surface restriction discovered on DL5 (`enable | disable | reset | reset-all | list`, no `get`). This makes the recon report self-documenting for the next testers. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (295→296, 1.2.37-beta→1.2.38-beta), `UpdateChecker.java` (1-line fix + 6 LoC explanatory comment), `dilink5/Dl5ClusterReconRunner.java` (8 dispatcher fixes in `runOne`, `probePlatform` signature change, `probeDisplays` + `probeClusterDisplayGeometry` rebuilt around dumpsys, `probePerApp` grep narrowed, new `shellSync` helper, updated catalog descriptions). No new permission, no manifest change, no new dependency, no protocol bump. **Zero behavioural regression** on production paths: the only runtime-visible change for end users is the OTA prompt now correctly says "up to date" once v1.2.38-beta is installed. The recon tab is purely diagnostic, fixes are catalog-internal. **What this release does NOT include** (deferred to v1.2.39+): live FORCE_RESIZE_APP wire-up (still gated on a clean recon-v2 run from at least one DL5 user confirming `am compat list ru.yandex.yandexmaps` reports the change ID is enablable from app uid 2000), and the proposed isolated live-test panel (Enable / Test launch / Reset buttons) — both deferred pending the next field log. |
 **DL5 cluster resize — dedicated recon tab + Telegram one-tap export.** Field log from a new DiLink 5 tester (Yandex Maps on cluster) confirmed that v1.2.35's revert of the global `force_resizable_activities` flag broke cluster resize on DL5 (Yandex Maps declares `resizeableActivity="false"` in its manifest → ATM coerces the activity to fullscreen at `realStartActivity` → `cmd activity task resize` becomes a silent no-op). Rather than re-flipping the global flag (which had system-wide side effects we explicitly reverted in v1.2.35), the chosen fix is the per-package compat override `am compat enable FORCE_RESIZE_APP <pkg>` (change ID 174042936) — but we must first confirm the compat framework shell surface is actually exposed on production BYD ROMs before shipping any live code path. **This release ships only the recon tooling**, no live cluster operation is wired yet. **New diagnostic tab "Cluster DL5"** (`activity_diag.xml` index 11, appended after Sniffer). The existing "Cluster" tab is renamed to **"Cluster DL3"** to make the platform split explicit (the old interactive POC panel `include_diag_cluster_poc.xml` is unchanged, only its tab label moves from `Cluster` to `Cluster DL3` via `diag_tab_cluster` string). New layout `include_diag_cluster_dl5.xml` (~140 LoC): NestedScrollView → header MaterialCardView (ic_science icon + title + subtitle + platform pill `tv_dl5_cluster_pill` + counters `tv_dl5_cluster_counters`) + action row (3 MaterialButtons: **Run recon** filled / **Copy report** outlined / **Send via Telegram** outlined) + programmatic test list `ll_dl5_cluster_test_list`. **New runner** `com.byd.dashcast.dilink5.Dl5ClusterReconRunner` (~380 LoC, reuses `DiLink5TestRunner.TestDef/TestResult/Status/Listener` types so DiagActivity row rendering is shared verbatim). Single-threaded ExecutorService `dl5-cluster-recon`, all listener callbacks marshalled to the UI thread via the main Looper. **Catalog: 30 recon-only tests** (no live cluster launch, no `am compat enable`, no settings mutation — pure probing): **R01–R05** platform identity (`Build.MODEL/PRODUCT/FINGERPRINT`, DisplayManager inventory with cluster auto-detection on name match `fission|xdja|cluster|virtual`, ADB local uid probe, SELinux enforce, cluster apps installed scan over Yandex Maps / Google Maps / Waze / Sygic / TomTom); **R06–R12** shell verb surface (`am`/`am start`/`cmd activity`/`cmd activity task`/`wm`/`cmd window`/`cmd display` — all via `2>&1 | head -N` to capture every `--help` line including `--display` / `--windowingMode` / `resize` / `set-ignore-orientation-request` / etc.); **R13–R18** compat framework (`am compat --help`, `am compat list | head -60`, then targeted `am compat get` against FORCE_RESIZE_APP / 174042936 / NEVER_SANDBOX_DISPLAY_APIS / OVERRIDE_MIN_ASPECT_RATIO scoped to our own package — this is the **critical block** that determines whether v1.2.38 can ship the per-app override fix); **R19–R23** global settings + dumpsys snapshots (`force_resizable_activities` current value, settings list global grep for `resizable|freeform|multi|sandbox|ignore_orientation`, dumpsys window/display/activity keyword filters); **R24–R25** wm geometry per-display (`wm size` + `wm density` on default display, then again on the detected cluster display); **R26–R28** per-app manifest probe via `pm dump <pkg> | grep -E 'resizeable\|maxAspectRatio\|launchMode\|targetSdk' | head -10` for Yandex Maps / Google Maps / Waze, with auto-SKIP when the app is not installed; **R29** service list filter for `auto_container|cluster|fission|xdja|window|activity_task`; **R30** `getprop` filter for `resizable|freeform|multi|sandbox|cluster|dilink|fission|xdja`. Each test runs through `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` synchronously (CountDownLatch via Object wait/notify, 6 s timeout per probe), captures stdout into the result detail, and PASS/WARN/FAIL classification based on presence of expected tokens. Total recon run < 20 s typical. **Telegram one-tap export.** New `AppLogger.shareReportToTelegram(Context, String)` (~65 LoC): probes 5 known Telegram package names in order (`org.telegram.messenger`, `org.telegram.messenger.web`, `org.telegram.plus`, `nekox.messenger`, `org.thunderdog.challegram`) via `PackageManager.getPackageInfo`, on hit fires a direct `ACTION_SEND` with `setPackage(hit)` + FileProvider URI (`byd_report_*.log` containing the report + the full in-memory AppLogger ringbuffer) + `EXTRA_STREAM` + `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION`. Three-level fallback chain: TG-found+file → direct intent; no-TG+file → generic chooser via `shareFile`; no-file → text-only chooser. The new "Send via Telegram" button in the Cluster DL5 panel wires straight to this helper, so the tester ships the full recon report back to the developer in one tap — no manual file-picking, no "share" submenu hunting. **DiagActivity wiring.** New constants `TAB_CLUSTER_DL5 = 11`, `TAB_COUNT = 12`. New fields: `panelClusterDl5`, `tvDl5ClusterSubtitle/Pill/Counters`, `btnDl5ClusterRunAll/CopyReport/SendTelegram`, `llDl5ClusterTestList`, `dl5ClusterRowViews`, `dl5ClusterLastResults`. New methods `bindClusterDl5Panel`, `prepareClusterDl5TestRows`, `runClusterDl5AllTests`, `updateClusterDl5Counters`, `copyClusterDl5Report`, `sendClusterDl5ToTelegram` (~110 LoC). Row binding delegates to the existing `bindDl5Row` since `Dl5ClusterReconRunner` shares the result types with `DiLink5TestRunner`. `showPanelForTab` extended to handle the new tab; `panelComingSoon` visibility condition extended. **Strings.** 15 new keys (`diag_tab_cluster_dl5`, `diag_dl5_cluster_header_title/subtitle`, `diag_dl5_cluster_pill_unknown/dl5/other`, `diag_dl5_cluster_run_all/copy_report/send_telegram`, `diag_dl5_cluster_counters_idle`, `diag_dl5_cluster_toast_report_copied/no_results/telegram_missing`), 1 modified (`diag_tab_cluster` value `Cluster` → `Cluster DL3`). French (default) + English shipped, other 10 locales fall back to fr/en (no `MissingTranslation` lint regression — the keys are tagged for the next i18n pass). **No live cluster operation.** Per explicit user instruction ("d'abord on fait le maximum de tests pour attraper les commandes disponibles, puis avec le retour on fera les tests etc."), this release is recon-only. Once the tester ships a report via Telegram and we confirm `am compat list` contains `FORCE_RESIZE_APP` (or its numeric ID 174042936) accessible from app uid 2000, v1.2.38 will wire the per-app fix into `ClusterService.startActivityViaShell` (chain `am compat enable 174042936 <pkg> ; am force-stop <pkg> ; am start --display N --windowingMode 5 ...`) with the existing global-flag path removed. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (294→295, 1.2.36-beta→1.2.37-beta), new `dilink5/Dl5ClusterReconRunner.java`, new `res/layout/include_diag_cluster_dl5.xml`, `AppLogger.java` (+`shareReportToTelegram`), `DiagActivity.java` (+import, +tab const, +panel binding, +methods), `activity_diag.xml` (+tab + panel include), `values/strings.xml` + `values-en/strings.xml` (rename + 15 new keys). No new permission, no manifest change, no protocol bump, no new dependency. **Zero behavioural regression** on DL2/DL3/DL4/DL5 production paths (the new tab is purely diagnostic, the rename `Cluster → Cluster DL3` is cosmetic and does not affect the panel id `panel_cluster_poc` nor any code path). |
| **1.2.36-beta** | 294 | **TetherFi-based Hotspot — full UI redesign + live stats + connected clients.** Hotspot screen rebuilt around the [TetherFi](https://github.com/pyamsoft/tetherfi) open-source proxy (no carrier tether quota / no BYD MIUI tether restrictions). New landscape 2-column layout (`activity_hotspot.xml` ~530 LoC): left hero card (status pill, update badge, Start/Stop/Toggle/Open TetherFi buttons); right column stacks Clients-and-Traffic card + Watchdog card + Autostart-on-boot card. New drawables `bg_hero_icon`, `bg_side_icon`, `bg_count_pill`, `ic_devices`, `ic_monitor_heart`, `ic_wifi_tethering`, `ic_sim_card`. **TetherFi integration** in `HotspotActivity.java`: only entry point is `com.pyamsoft.tetherfi/.activity.ProxyTileActivity` with `key_action` ∈ START/STOP/TOGGLE (no reflection, no private APIs). Independent stats probe (`statsTick`, 5 s) decoupled from the watchdog so stats keep working when auto-restart is OFF; probes `dumpsys activity services com.pyamsoft.tetherfi | grep ProxyForegroundService` via `AdbLocalClient`. Session lifecycle (`onProbeTransition`) snapshots `SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()` + TetherFi UID (cached via PackageManager) + `TrafficStats.getUidRxBytes/getUidTxBytes` baselines on DOWN→UP; resets to -1 on UP→DOWN. **Connected clients enumeration**: combined ADB payload `dumpsys wifip2p 2>/dev/null; echo '===ARP==='; cat /proc/net/arp 2>/dev/null`; static `parseClients(String)` splits on the separator, builds MAC→IPv4 map from `arp`, walks the `wifip2p` dump for `Client list` / `mClients` / `clients:` / `wifip2pdevice` sections and pairs each MAC with the nearest `deviceName=` token; static inner type `HClient { mac, name, ip }` + regex `P_MAC` / `P_NAME`. `renderClients(List<HClient>)` updates count pill, populates `ll_clients_list` via programmatic `buildClientRow(c)` (icon + name TextView weight=1 + monospace IP TextView), toggles empty state. Per-client RX/TX intentionally not shown (kernel does not expose per-MAC counters without root). **TetherFi update checker** (`TetherFiUpdateChecker.java`): background HEAD/JSON probe against TetherFi GitHub Releases at boot, compares installed APK version against `tag_name`, caches result in SharedPreferences; hotspot card reads it on resume and surfaces an "Update available" badge with a link button. Pure best-effort, all failures swallowed silently. **Boot autostart**: `BootReceiver` extended to fire `ProxyTileActivity` with `key_action=START` when the new pref `hotspot_autostart_on_boot` is true; also kicks the update checker once. **Strings**: 40+ new keys for the redesigned screen, both `fr` (default) and `en` localized (`hotspot_clients_title`, `hotspot_clients_empty`, `hotspot_stat_uptime/rx/tx`, etc.). **Misc**: small wiring tweaks in `MainActivity`, `SettingsActivity`, `activity_main.xml`, `activity_settings.xml` to surface the hotspot entry point with the new card style; minor helper added in `Platform.java`. **Known limitations**: per-client bandwidth not supported (root required); client list only shows devices currently in the ARP table (peers without recent IP traffic may briefly be missing); update checker requires internet at boot, otherwise the badge appears on the next launch with connectivity. No new permissions, no manifest protocol bump. |
| **1.2.32-build223** | 223 | **DL5 resize — Fix #4 : neutralisation système du flag manifeste `resizeableActivity="false"` via `force_resizable_activities` + force-stop avant chaque launch cluster.** Le field log `log/BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_194219.txt` (build 222 installée chez le testeur Dilink5 Yandex Maps) confirme que les trois fixes v1.2.30 atterrissent correctement : (i) `am start --display 3 --windowingMode 5` est honoré par ATM, qui crée la **root task** en `windowingMode=5` (ligne `getOrCreateRootTask windowingMode=5, activityType=1` au launch L15212), (ii) les bounds sont bien exprimées dans l'espace framebuffer du display 3 (`cluster FREEFORM bounds=Rect(134, 174 - 1786, 906) display=3 1920×1080` L15117), (iii) la sonde VERIFY post-resize est dispatchée et `cmd activity task resize 97 134 174 1786 906` retourne `exit=0` (L16040). **MAIS** le VERIFY dumpsys montre que l'activité elle-même se retrouve immédiatement coercée en `mWindowingMode=fullscreen mBounds=Rect(0, 0 - 1920, 1080)` (L16311+). **Cause racine — quatrième couche du problème.** Yandex Maps (comme la majorité des apps de navigation : Yandex Navi, Google Maps, Sygic, Waze, etc.) déclare `android:resizeableActivity="false"` dans son manifeste pour bloquer le split-screen. Sur API 32 cette propriété est respectée à la lettre par ActivityTaskManagerService : une activité non-resizable **ne peut pas** entrer en freeform, peu importe le windowing mode de la root task parent. ATM force la transition vers fullscreen au moment du `realStartActivity`, et `cmd activity task resize` devient ensuite un **silent no-op** sur cette task (retourne `exit=0` sans modifier les bounds — exact symptôme observé). **Fix.** Activation programmatique du toggle Dev Option « Force activities to be resizable » (clé persistante `Settings.Global.force_resizable_activities`). Quand ce flag vaut 1, ATM ignore le manifest `resizeableActivity` de toutes les apps et les traite uniformément comme resizable, autorisant freeform + bounds resize à s'appliquer. La modif se fait via `settings put global force_resizable_activities 1` exécuté à travers le shell uid 2000 d'`AdbLocalClient` (uid shell détient `WRITE_SECURE_SETTINGS` system-wide, perm signature/privileged inaccessible à l'uid app 10148). Le flag est persistant ; le re-poser à chaque launch est idempotent et auto-correcteur si un autre process ou un reset de Settings le passait à 0. **Force-stop préalable.** ATM évalue `force_resizable_activities` au moment de la création de la task, pas en cours de vie d'un process existant. Pour qu'une app déjà lancée picke le nouveau réglage, il faut tuer son process puis le relancer. Ajout de `am force-stop <pkg>` entre le `settings put` et le `am start --display N --windowingMode 5 ...`. Conséquence : le launch cluster sur DL5 est désormais déterministe sur toute app, y compris celles qui posent `resizeableActivity="false"`. **VERIFY probe affinée.** Le sed/awk précédent dumpait `mGlobalConfig` (une entrée par Configuration carrier dans l'arbre activities), puis `head -20` tronquait AVANT d'atteindre la vraie stanza de la task. Remplacé par un range awk `/Task=Task\{[^}]*#N[ }]/,/Task=Task\{[^}]*#[0-9]+[ }]/` qui isole précisément la stanza de la task ciblée, suivi d'un grep sur les seuls champs qui comptent (`mBounds`, `WindowingMode`, `displayId`, `resizeMode`, `#N`). Beaucoup plus de signal, plus de bruit `mGlobalConfig`. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (222→223), `ClusterService.java` (`startActivityViaShell` : chain shell `settings put + am force-stop + am start` avec bloc commentaire `v1.2.32 — DL5 resize second-order fix` ~30 LoC ; `resizeActiveTask` DL5 branch : VERIFY probe stanza-targeted ~10 LoC). Aucune nouvelle permission, aucun nouveau resource, aucun bump de protocole, aucune nouvelle dépendance. Aucun changement de comportement sur DL3 / DL4 / DL2 (la branche est dans `startActivityViaShell` qui n'est appelée que par le chemin `isDiLink5`). **Contrat comportemental.** DL5 testeur — install v1.2.32 par-dessus v1.2.31, relancer projection cluster, ouvrir Yandex Maps sur cluster via DashCast : visuellement, l'app Yandex Maps doit désormais respecter les marges H/V configurées et apparaître inscrite dans le rectangle `(insetH, insetV, fb_w-insetH, fb_h-insetV)` du framebuffer cluster, et non plein-cadre. Field log doit montrer `am force-stop ru.yandex.yandexmaps` suivi de `am start --display 3 --windowingMode 5 ...` puis VERIFY avec `mBounds=Rect(134, 174 - 1786, 906)` et `mWindowingMode=freeform` (au lieu de fullscreen). DL3 / DL4 / DL2 : aucun chemin nouveau exercé. |

| **1.2.30-build221** | 221 | **DL5 resize root cause fix — trois correctifs en cascade dans `ClusterService.java`.** Diagnostic déclenché par le field log DL5 `log/BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_184727.txt` (utilisateur Yandex Maps, build 219 installée) : l'app est bien lancée sur display 3 (XDJAScreenProjection_0, taskId=49 confirmé), `cmd activity task resize 49 374 275 1546 445` retourne `exit=0` systématiquement… mais la dalle cluster (display 2 physique 1920×720) reste full bleed sans la moindre marge visible. Trois causes étroitement imbriquées trouvées, fixées ensemble. **Fix #1 — `startActivityViaShell()` ajoute `--windowingMode 5`.** La commande shell était `am start --display 3 -a MAIN -c LAUNCHER -n pkg/cls --activity-clear-task` — sans aucun flag de windowing mode, la task atterrissait en `WINDOWING_MODE_FULLSCREEN` (=1). Or `cmd activity task resize` est un **silent no-op sur une task FULLSCREEN depuis API 30+** (la méthode interne détecte le mode et bail sans erreur ; AOSP `ActivityTaskManagerService.resizeTask` exige FREEFORM ou FREEFORM_PIP). Ajout de `--windowingMode 5` (flag `am start` officiel AOSP depuis API 26, toujours présent en API 32) entre `--display N` et `-a MAIN`. La task lance directement en FREEFORM, les bounds passent. Le fait que `applyClusterFreeformBounds()` calculait déjà `ActivityOptions.setLaunchWindowingMode(5)` + `setLaunchBounds(…)` ne servait à rien sur DL5 puisqu'on by-passe `startActivityViaIAM()` au profit du shell (SecurityException IATM uid 10148) — les ActivityOptions étaient construites puis jetées. **Fix #2 — `resizeActiveTask()` exprime les bounds dans l'espace framebuffer du display 3.** Le log révèle `cluster FREEFORM bounds=Rect(374, 275 - 1546, 805) display=3 1920×1080` au launch (via `d.getRealSize()` sur display 3 = 1080) MAIS `bounds=Rect(374, 275 - 1546, 445)` au resize (via `mInputForwarder.getClusterHeight()` = 720, hardcodé côté `ClusterInputForwarder.mClusterHeight`). Deux espaces de coordonnées différents. La task vit dans le **framebuffer du display 3 (1920×1080)**, pas dans l'espace de la dalle physique (1920×720) — c'est Qt côté natif qui scale 1080→720 vers la dalle via la Surface consumée par le TextureView XDJA. `resizeTask` applique les bounds dans l'espace de la display du task, donc il faut 1080 sinon on shrink dans un buffer déjà plus grand. Le code interroge maintenant directement `DisplayManager.getDisplay(clusterId).getRealSize()` au moment du resize (fallback `mInputForwarder.getClusterWidth/Height()` si la détection échoue, pour préserver le comportement DL3). Sur DL3 cette même logique renvoie 1920×720 (display 1 = la dalle elle-même), aucun changement de comportement. Conséquence : les marges H/V que l'utilisateur saisit sont interprétées dans l'espace framebuffer ; sur DL5 elles seront ensuite légèrement compressées verticalement par Qt (facteur 720/1080 = 0.667 sur V), mais visuellement cohérentes. **Fix #3 — sonde de vérification dumpsys post-resize.** Après succès de `cmd activity task resize`, on dispatche immédiatement `dumpsys activity activities | grep -E 'taskId=N|Task=…#N|mBounds|getWindowingMode|windowingMode='` (très filtré pour rester sous le quota AdbLocal). Le résultat est loggé en INFO préfixé « resizeActiveTask VERIFY ». Permet de confirmer en field log que (a) la task est bien en `windowingMode=5` (FREEFORM) après notre `am start --windowingMode 5`, et (b) que `mBounds` reflète exactement le `Rect(insetH, insetV, fbW - insetH, fbH - insetV)` qu'on a passé. Si ces deux invariants sont confirmés mais que rien n'apparait visuellement, la prochaine investigation portera sur la couche Qt/XDJA (le TextureView consommateur de la Surface display 3 peut filtrer ou stretcher différemment). **Compatibilité plateformes.** DL3 (Seal EU API 29) : (i) Fix #1 inactif (DL3 utilise `startActivityViaIAM()`, pas le shell DL5), (ii) Fix #2 transparent (`getRealSize()` sur display 1 = 1920×720 = identique à `getClusterHeight()`), (iii) Fix #3 inactif (DL3 utilise la branche `else` legacy `am task resize`). DL2 (HMI seul) : aucun cluster display → `clusterId<=0` → méthode bail upfront, aucun chemin touché. DL4 : si jamais détecté via Platform, même logique que DL3. Zero régression possible sur les plateformes hors DL5. **Build.** Compilé sans erreur ni warning nouveau. À valider en voiture par l'utilisateur DL5 sur Yandex Maps : ouvrir l'app sur cluster avec des marges H=100/V=80 déjà définies, observer si la zone affichée est réellement réduite, puis récupérer un log `byd_log_*.log` pour lire les lignes `resizeActiveTask VERIFY` (windowingMode + mBounds effectifs après ATM). |

**dialog cleanup** (titre, message, bouton confirmer, status fmt, toast fmt, busy fmt) ; **chooser title export** ; **warnings Settings** (DL2 no margins, no cluster, error prefix fmt, no browser fmt). **Wiring.** `include_diag_adas.xml` : 7 `android:text="..."` littéraux → `@string/diag_adas_*`. `include_diag_sniffer.xml` : 11 littéraux → `@string/diag_sniffer_*`. `activity_diag.xml` : `android:text="Sniffer"` → `@string/diag_tab_sniffer`. `DiagActivity.java` : 13 setText/Toast/AlertDialog avec littéraux français → `getString(R.string...)` / `setText(R.string...)` / `setTitle(R.string...)`. `SettingsActivity.java` : 3 setText/Toast (2 warnings DL2 + prefix erreur overscan + Toast aucun navigateur) → `R.string.settings_*`. **Argument formats identiques** dans toutes les langues (`%1$s`, `%2$d`, `%1$d`, etc.) pour zéro risque d’IllegalFormatException runtime. **Garanties anti-régression.** Aucune logique modifiée : ce sont uniquement des extractions mécaniques de chaînes. Les commandes shell, le binder `AutoContainer`/`auto_container`, le tagging `BYD_RE_Sniffer_*`, la persistance `SharedPreferences`, le cycle setsid — strictement inchangés. Aucune clé supprimée ou renommée ; uniquement des ajouts. Lint Android validera `MissingTranslation` zéro warning. Build passé avec succès sans warning nouveau. **Chaînes intentionnellement non extraites.** Les 3 textes techniques longs (corps « Détails techniques » dans ADAS, « Contenu capturé » et « Procédure type » dans Sniffer) restent en français dans les layouts — ce sont des références de reverse engineering (commandes shell, codes binder, noms de processus) qui n’ont pas d’équivalent UX dans d’autres langues ; leur traduction dégraderait la lisibilité technique. Le label statique « Auto_container » dans le dropdown ADAS est aussi conservé (nom de service binder, identique partout). Les setText fingerprints (`"Build " + disp + " · "`, `"v" + versionName`) dans `SysInfoActivity` et `WelcomeActivity` restent littéraux (conventions de versionning techniques, identiques en toutes langues). Le Toast `"✓"` dans `MainActivity` reste un simple checkmark Unicode. **Total cumulé par locale** : 289 → 335 chaînes traduites (égalité stricte avec `values/` minus les 3 OTA non-translatables — toujours intentionnel). |

| **1.2.27-build218** | 218 | **DL4 — auto-detection of `BYD-AUTO/DiLink4.0` (API 29) + hard neutralisation of FORCE_ON DiLink 5 override on DL4 hardware.** Field log `BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_175751.txt` from a *new* testeur on a different car proved the cluster never activated: every projection attempt logged `Service auto_container does not exist` (11+ repeats across `log/byd_log_20260523_173033.log.bin`, `log/byd_report_20260523_172928.log.bin`, `log/byd_report_20260523_173322.log.bin`). **Root cause.** The car is a genuine DiLink 4.0 (`ro.product.name=DiLink4.0`, `Build.MODEL=DiLink4.0 For BYD AUTO`, `Build.FINGERPRINT=BYD-AUTO/DiLink4.0/DiLink4.0:10/QKQ1.210218.001/…`, API 29, hardware=qcom), but the user had manually flipped the « DiLink 5 » switch in Settings (FORCE_ON) — possibly because previous releases targeted DL5 heavily so the option sounded like the right one to try. With FORCE_ON enabled, `Platform.isDiLink5(ctx)` returned `true` → `AdbLocalClient.autoContainerSvcName` returned the snake_case `auto_container` binder name (DL5 ABI) → every `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 16 s16 ""` was rejected by the platform because on DL3/DL4 the binder is registered as the PascalCase `AutoContainer` (confirmed by Beta Engine 28/28 PASS in `byd_report_20260523_172928.log.bin`: P6 *AutoContainer reachable, descriptor=android.os.IAutoContainer (transact ret=true)*, P13 *sendInfo(1000,30,"") via transact(2) on android.os.IAutoContainer (ret=true)*). Result: 0 PRESENTATION display ever materialised, every `am start --display -1` landed on the HMI itself, the user saw nothing on the cluster. **Fix.** `Platform` now (a) auto-detects DiLink 4 from fingerprint/product/model (substring match on `dilink4` / `dilink_4` / `dilink 4`) **gated by API 28-29** so a future ROM mis-named « DiLink4 » on Android 12 cannot be mis-routed; (b) exposes a new `isDiLink4(Context)` accessor (no user override — DL4 detection is purely automatic, same contract as the existing `isDiLink2`); (c) **hard-neutralises** the FORCE_ON DL5 override on any device where `autoDiLink4 == true`: `isDiLink5(ctx)` returns `false` unconditionally before reading the override, with `describeMode(ctx)` reporting `AUTO=off (DL4 detected — DL5 FORCE_ON ignored)` so the Settings panel surfaces the absorbed mistake to power users. Downstream code is auto-rerouted with zero changes: `AdbLocalClient.autoContainerSvcName` falls back to the CamelCase `AutoContainer` (already its non-DL5 default), `ClusterManager.activateClusterDisplay` falls back to the DL3 path (sendInfo 30 → 6 s → sendInfo 16 → 6 s → sendInfo 35 → poll DisplayManager for `fission_bg_xdjaVirtualSurface`). **Why this is conservative.** No new permission, no manifest change, no new dependency, no behavioural change on DL3 (Seal EU / non-DL4 BYD) or DL5 cars — only DL4 hardware sees the neutralisation, and only after positive fingerprint+API match. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (217→218, 1.2.26→1.2.27), `platform/Platform.java` (+`autoDiLink4` field, +`detectDiLink4(...)` static, +`isAutoDetectedDiLink4()` accessor, +`isDiLink4(Context)` accessor, rewrote `isDiLink5(Context)` guard + `describeMode(Context)`, ~40 LoC net). **Validation plan.** (a) DL4 testeur (the user who reported this issue) — install v1.2.27, launch projection from the dashboard → cluster shows `clusterdebug` (or whatever default cluster target the build ships with) within 12-13 s of the tap. Logcat should show `ClusterManager: activateClusterDisplay (path=DL3 fast/full)` and **never** `Service auto_container does not exist`. Re-toggling « DiLink 5 » FORCE_ON in Settings must have **zero observable effect** on cluster activation (the guard absorbs it). (b) Settings → DL5 toggle subtitle shows `AUTO=off (DL4 detected — DL5 FORCE_ON ignored)` when FORCE_ON is set; `AUTO=off (DL4 detected)` when AUTO or FORCE_OFF. (c) Seal EU DL3 testeur — identical behaviour to v1.2.26 (DL4 detection requires the `dilink4` substring + API 28-29; Seal EU is API 29 but fingerprint does not contain `dilink4` so the new path is never taken). (d) DL5 testeur — identical behaviour to v1.2.26 (DL5 fingerprint never contains `dilink4`, autoDiLink4 stays false). **Follow-up (v1.2.28+ if requested).** A dedicated `TEST DL4` diagnostic suite (probing sendInfo 30/16/35 sequentially with DisplayManager dumps between each step, modelled on the existing `DiLink5TestRunner`/`DiLink2TestRunner` pattern) is on standby — not shipped in this release to keep the patch surgical and unblock the DL4 testeur immediately. |
| **1.2.26-build217** | 217 | **DL5 — cluster app resize: skip `am task resize` (silent no-op on API 30+) and shell straight to `cmd activity task resize`.** Field log `BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_172007.txt` of v1.2.25 (which fixed the typing path end-to-end — user confirmed « ENFIN ! ») surfaced that the cluster-app resize UI in MainActivity (Apply Resize button + per-app autoApplyInsets) was a no-op on DL5. Every press produced a clean 4-line trace pair, repeated 11 times for Yandex Maps taskId=38 (e.g. `bounds=Rect(206, 91 - 1714, 629)` at 17:21:15.717): `W ClusterService: resizeActiveTask reflection failed: SecurityException: Permission Denial: resizeTask() from pid=22748, uid=10148 requires android.permission.MANAGE_ACTIVITY_TASKS` → `D AdbLocalClient: am task resize 38 206 91 1714 629 2>&1; echo "exit=$?" -> exit=0` → `I ClusterService: resizeActiveTask 'am task resize' -> "exit=0" (looksOk=true)` — and the cluster visibly **did not resize**. **Root cause.** The v1.2.18 code comment already flagged that `am task resize` on AOSP API 30+ « often returns empty stdout (apparent success) but no visible effect » (the `am` binary was rewritten and most `am task` subcommands became silent shells around `cmd activity task …`). v1.2.18 added a `cmd activity task resize` fallback but gated it on a `looksOk` heuristic that treats `exit=0` (with no `Unknown command` / `error` / `exception` substring) as success. On DL5 (API 32) `am task resize` deterministically returns `exit=0` → `looksOk=true` → the fallback was never reached, ever. **Fix.** `ClusterService.resizeActiveTask` now branches on `AdbLocalClient.isDiLink5Safe(this)` after the reflection path's expected `SecurityException` (no `MANAGE_ACTIVITY_TASKS` for app uid 10148). On DL5 it skips `am task resize` entirely and dispatches `cmd activity task resize <taskId> <l> <t> <r> <b> 2>&1; echo "exit=$?"` (the canonical AOSP API 30+ verb, reached via the same uid=shell pipe that already executes `pm grant`, `service call AutoContainer`, `settings put secure …`). On DL3 (API 29) the legacy `am task resize` → fallback chain is preserved verbatim — `am` still works on Android 10. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (216→217, 1.2.25→1.2.26), `ClusterService.java` (~30 LoC in `resizeActiveTask`: extracted the DL5 branch that dispatches `cmd activity task resize` directly; kept the legacy DL3 chain in an `else`; expanded the inline comment to document the v1.2.26 field-log evidence). No new permissions, no manifest change, no new strings, no new dependency, no protocol bump. **Behavioural contract.** DL5: drag overscan sliders in MainActivity (or the global Settings sliders → auto-applied per-app via `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded`) → press Apply → `cmd activity task resize` fires with the computed bounds → cluster app shrinks/expands within the safe area in ≤ 1 frame. DL3 (`am task resize` is healthy on API 29 there): no behavioural change, legacy path executes as before. DL2: cluster path doesn't exist (zero impact). **Validation plan.** (a) DL5 testeur — install v1.2.26 over v1.2.25, launch Yandex Maps on cluster, open MainActivity, move H/V sliders to e.g. 200/100, press Apply → cluster Yandex visibly indents by 200 px H / 100 px V. (b) `logcat | grep ClusterService` should show `resizeActiveTask DL5: dispatching 'cmd activity task resize' (skipping 'am task resize' — known silent no-op on API 30+)` then `resizeActiveTask 'cmd activity task resize' -> "exit=0"` with no further `am task` line. (c) Press Reset Insets → cluster Yandex returns to full 1920×720 within a frame. (d) DL3 — typing app keyboard sliders still work as before (legacy `am task resize` path). |
| **1.2.25-build216** | 216 | **DL5 — KeyboardBridge: don't force a second tap after ADB enable + reach the cluster window via `getWindowsOnAllDisplays()`.** Field log `BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_170436.txt` of v1.2.24 (which fixed the ANR / Back-button-freeze) surfaced two follow-on bugs reported by the user: (1) every time the user tapped the ⌨ icon the « DashCast Cluster IME ✓ » Toast appeared and forced them to tap a second time before they could type; (2) typing in the bridge produced zero text on the cluster Yandex search field — only `W ClusterImeWatcher: setTextOnCluster no-op: no focused editable on cluster` lines (at 17:05:14.156 and 17:05:28.765). **(Root cause — Bug 1.)** v1.2.23's `tryAdbEnableA11y` success branch called `finish()` after the `OK:…` reply, on the design that the user would retap the ⌨ icon and find the freshly bound service ready. But the field log proves `AccessibilityManagerService` rebinds `ClusterImeWatcherService` within ~30 ms of the `settings put` write — `onServiceConnected — watching cluster focus events (DL5=true)` arrives at 17:05:02.243, BEFORE the success callback at 17:05:02.272. So the bridge already has IME focus, the soft keyboard is up, the worker `sInstance` is alive — there is nothing to wait for. Finishing the bridge here is pure user friction. The « toast every time » perception is because the OS revokes a11y enablement on every package update (`AccessibilityManagerService.onPackageUpdateFinished`) as a standard Android security measure, so after each install the secure setting really did contain no entry for our service. With `finish()` removed the user goes through the enable Toast exactly once after each install, then keeps typing in the same bridge instance. **(Root cause — Bug 2.)** `findClusterFocusedEditable()` called `AccessibilityService.getWindows()` — which on every Android API returns **only windows on the default display**, even with `FLAG_RETRIEVE_INTERACTIVE_WINDOWS`. The cluster Yandex search EditText lives on display 2 (composed face) / display 3 (shadow framebuffer driven by the XDJA fission compositor), neither of which is the default. So the window walk found exactly zero candidates and the worker logged `no focused editable on cluster` on every keystroke. **Fix.** `findClusterFocusedEditable()` now uses `AccessibilityService.getWindowsOnAllDisplays()` (API 30+, DL5 ships API 32) — returns `SparseArray<List<AccessibilityWindowInfo>>` keyed by displayId, exactly what we need — and iterates every display, preferring `displayId != 0` for the « bestNonDefault » slot. Falls back to the single-display walk on older APIs (DL3 stays gated by `isDiLink5` upstream so this is just belt-and-braces). Also factored the per-window focus extraction into a static `pickFocusedEditableFrom(AccessibilityWindowInfo, String selfPkg)` helper which explicitly **skips windows from our own package** — necessary because v1.2.25 keeps the bridge open after the ADB success, so the bridge's own EditText would otherwise compete for `findFocus(FOCUS_INPUT)` and win on display 0, causing us to push the user's keystrokes back into the local field instead of the cluster Yandex search. **(Touched files.)** `app/build.gradle` (215→216, 1.2.24→1.2.25), `KeyboardBridgeActivity.java` (~5 LoC: removed `finish()` from the ADB success branch; expanded the inline comment to document why the bridge must stay open), `ime/ClusterImeWatcherService.java` (~110 LoC: rewrote `findClusterFocusedEditable` around `getWindowsOnAllDisplays` + extracted `pickFocusedEditableFrom` helper with self-package filter). No new permissions, no new strings, no manifest change, no new dependency, no protocol bump. **(Behavioural contract.)** DL5 with a11y already enabled (steady-state after this release): tap ⌨ → bridge opens → IME pops up → no Toast (the `isClusterImeWatcherEnabled` check returns true) → typing routes to cluster Yandex immediately. DL5 right after install: tap ⌨ → bridge opens → IME pops up → Toast `✓` (ADB enabled the service) → user keeps typing in the same bridge → routes. DL3/DL2: gated by `isDiLink5`, zero behavioural change. **(Validation plan.)** (a) DL5 testeur — install v1.2.25 over v1.2.24, tap ⌨ ONCE → bridge opens + IME up + Toast `✓` once → type « test » → cluster Yandex search shows « test » within a frame → tap IME Enter → search executes. (b) Tap ⌨ a second time later: bridge opens + IME up, NO Toast, typing routes immediately. (c) Logcat — search for `setTextOnCluster no-op: no focused editable on cluster`: should be silent during normal typing; only emitted if the cluster app has no focused EditText (i.e. user opened the bridge with no search field in focus on the cluster). Search for `setTextOnCluster ACTION_SET_TEXT refused`: would indicate the cluster app actively rejected the action (rare, per-app workaround needed). |
| **1.2.24-build215** | 215 | **DL5 — `ClusterImeWatcherService.setTextOnCluster` / `performImeEnterOnCluster` moved off the UI thread (fixes the ANR that killed the app after v1.2.23 ADB-enable succeeded).** Field log `BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_164557.txt` of v1.2.23 produced an excellent positive trace at 16:46:18.804 — `I KeyboardBridge: tryAdbEnableA11y SUCCESS: OK:com.byd.airconditioning/.gesture.AcGestureService:com.android.systemui/.custom.StatusBarAccessibilityService:com.byd.autovoice/…/SceneSayService:com.byd.dashcast/com.byd.dashcast.ime.ClusterImeWatcherService` (the colon-separated preservation worked, BYD's own services intact + ours appended) immediately followed by `I ClusterImeWatcher: onServiceConnected — watching cluster focus events (DL5=true)` (AccessibilityManagerService rebound our service on the next tick, exactly as v1.2.23's design predicted). The user saw the `DashCast Cluster IME ✓` Toast, retapped ⌨, IME came up. Then everything fell apart: typed characters never reached Yandex on the cluster, and tactile **Back was unresponsive for 17-18 seconds**. Logcat smoking gun: `I am_anr (991): [0,15738,com.byd.dashcast,…,Input dispatching timed out … (server) is not responding. Waited 5000ms for KeyEvent]` at 16:46:36.233, followed by the system-wide SIGQUIT thread-dump fan-out, then `E ActivityManager: ANR in com.byd.dashcast (com.byd.dashcast/.KeyboardBridgeActivity)` at 16:46:47.206, then `am_proc_died(991): [0,15738,com.byd.dashcast,0,2]` — and the main-thread stack in the tombstone contained `at com.byd.dashcast.ime.ClusterImeWatcherService.setTextOnCluster(ClusterImeWatcherService.java:256)`. **Root cause.** v1.2.12's `setTextOnCluster()` ran fully synchronously on the caller's thread — and the caller was `KeyboardBridgeActivity`'s `TextWatcher.afterTextChanged`, i.e. the main UI thread. The body calls `findClusterFocusedEditable()`, which iterates `getWindows()` and runs `root.findFocus(FOCUS_INPUT)` against every interactive window — including the cluster windows on display 3, which round-trips through `AccessibilityManagerService` over Binder. With `FLAG_RETRIEVE_INTERACTIVE_WINDOWS` and a fission compositor topology this walk can take dozens of ms per window; a fast typist queues N walks back-to-back on the main thread; the next KeyEvent dispatch waits ≥ 5 000 ms → ANR → process killed → the user's subsequent Back taps land on nothing for the 17-18 s until the system finishes the SIGQUIT dump + process restart; after restart the mirror touch pipeline is in a transient state until the daemon reconnects (visible in the same log as the `attemptStartMirror : service non disponible` repeats around 16:46:55). The 3 user-reported symptoms (text not arriving, Back frozen, mirror touch broken after relaunch) all collapse to this single root cause. **Fix.** `ClusterImeWatcherService` now owns a private `HandlerThread "cluster-ime-relay"` started in `onCreate` (BACKGROUND priority), torn down in `onDestroy` via `removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)` + `quitSafely()`. **(1) `setTextOnCluster(CharSequence)`** is now strictly non-blocking — it stores the latest text in `volatile mPendingText`, removes any in-flight `mSetTextRunner`, and reposts it with **SET_TEXT_DEBOUNCE_MS = 80 ms** coalescing window so a typist filling « apple street » fires the a11y tree walk + `ACTION_SET_TEXT` at most ~12 times/second, last-writer-wins. Returns `true` optimistically (failures logged inside the runnable, no caller-thread blocking ever). The runnable itself runs the original v1.2.12 logic (`findClusterFocusedEditable` → `performAction(ACTION_SET_TEXT, args)`) on the worker thread. **(2) `performImeEnterOnCluster()`** also dispatches to the worker; it first cancels the pending debounced setText, then on the worker thread synchronously runs `mSetTextRunner` (so Enter sees the final text, not a half-debounced view), then walks the focus once and fires `ACTION_IME_ENTER` (API 30+) or `ACTION_CLICK` (fallback). Returns `true` immediately for the same non-blocking contract. **(3) Worker lifecycle.** Worker is created on each `onCreate`/destroyed on each `onDestroy`, never crosses service-rebind boundaries (matches the existing `sInstance` lifecycle). Belt-and-braces: both helpers null-check `mWorker` before posting (covers a vanishingly narrow window between `onCreate` failure and rebind). **(4) Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (214→215, 1.2.23→1.2.24), `ime/ClusterImeWatcherService.java` (+`mWorkerThread/mWorker/mPendingText/mSetTextRunner` fields, `onCreate` spins up the worker, `onDestroy` tears it down, `setTextOnCluster` rewritten to non-blocking dispatch + debounce, `performImeEnterOnCluster` rewritten to worker-dispatched flush-then-enter, ~90 LoC net). No new permissions, no new strings, no manifest change, no new dependency. The on-cluster typing UX remains identical from the user's perspective (text appears in the cluster field as they type, Enter executes search) — only the threading model changes. **(5) Behavioural contract.** DL3 / DL2: bridge entry point still gated by `isDiLink5` (path never exercised), zero behavioural change.
