Same pattern as DL5 D9. Cleared at the start of each run so re-running the suite gives a fresh capture. PASS when ≥1 match. **S17 — APK extraction → `/storage/emulated/0/Download/dl2_apks/`.** THE headline test of this build. Mirrors DL5 D10's shell-based extraction (avoids app-uid sandbox issues on /sdcard since A10) but with two DL2-specific tweaks: (a) output dir is `dl2_apks/` (visually distinct from DL5's `dashcast_apks/` in the same Download folder), (b) **`com.byd.cluster` is sorted FIRST in the extraction order** via a custom `Collections.sort` so even if the test runs out of time the most-RE-relevant APK is captured first. Whitelist (`S17_INTERESTING`): `com.byd.cluster` (priority #1), `com.byd.appstartmanagement`, `com.byd.providers.appstartup`, `com.byd.containerservice`, `com.xdja.containerservice`, `com.byd.clusterdebug`, `com.byd.car.server`, `com.byd.crosscontrol`, `com.byd.smarttravel`, `com.byd.commander`, `com.byd.freedom`, `com.byd.overdrive`, `com.byd.windowmanagement` + substring tokens `fission`, `projection`, `cluster`, `dilink`, `magicwindow`, `mirror`. Extraction via `cat '<src>' > '<dst>'` shell redirect (uid 2000 has write to `/sdcard/Download/` on A9+), cache via `stat -c %s` to skip already-extracted APKs (filename embeds `versionCode`). On second run with no new packages, S17 produces zero `cat` calls. Status: PASS if ok>0 && fail==0, WARN if mixed, FAIL if no APK extracted. Message reads `N APK(s) in Download/dl2_apks/ (X cached), Y skipped`. SKIPPED if S16 was never run. The target path matches the user's screenshot showing the device file manager browsing `/storage/emulated/0/Download` — direct access from the BYD file picker, no `adb pull` needed. **S18 — `dumpsys window` focused/displays/overscan.** `dumpsys window | grep -E 'mDisplayId|mCurrentFocus|mFocusedApp|mDisplayContent|imeLayerStack|mStackId|mOverscan' | head -60`. Snapshot of WindowManager's view of the focused activity, per-display content roots, IME layer stack, and overscan rects — useful baseline before reproducing any display bug. **S19 — `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger` topology.** `--list` (top 30 layers) + filtered topology (`Display |layerStack|Output|orientation|HWComposer|hwc composition`). Ground-truth physical/virtual display map from the compositor side, bypasses WindowManager filtering. **S20 — Recent BYD logcat.** `logcat -d -v threadtime | grep -iE '<BYD/cluster/projection keywords>' | tail -200`. Captures the persistent buffer's last ~200 BYD/cluster log lines, surfaces silent errors (SecurityException stacks, binder denials) that don't reach UI. **S21 — `com.byd.cluster` full dumpsys package.** Shell-level manifest dump (250 lines): activities/services/receivers/providers with `exported` flag, declared + requested permissions with `protectionLevel`, signature, install location. Complements L11's PackageManager-only view (PM filters some fields for non-system app uids on API 28). Status WARN with `not installed on this DL2 device` when the package is absent. **S22 — `com.byd.appstartmanagement` full dumpsys package.** Same depth as S21 for the second BYD package of interest. On DL5 this package gates app launch routing; if it has the same role on DL2 it's the second mandatory RE target. **S23 — BYD framework JARs + permission XMLs probe.** `ls -la /system/framework/`, `/vendor/framework/`, `/system/etc/permissions/`, `/vendor/etc/permissions/` all filtered on `byd|alps|cluster|automotive`. Surfaces vendor SDK platform JARs (e.g. `byd-services.jar`, `byd_carapi.xml`) needed to understand the framework API surface available to BYD apps. **S24 — `am stack list` / `am task list` (API 28).** Both subcommands still ship on API 28 (removed in API 30+). Enumerates ActivityManager stacks and tasks with their `displayId` — first concrete evidence of whether DL2 actually has tasks living on any display other than 0. Status PASS if any `Stack id=` or `taskId=` line is present. **S25 — Crash logs.** `ls -lt /data/tombstones/`, `/data/anr/`, `/data/system/dropbox/` (last filtered on `byd|cluster|crash|anr`). Lists the 10 most recent native crashes / ANRs / BYD-relevant dropbox entries. Status WARN with `crash artefacts present` when any crash file is detected; useful to spot silent cluster process crashes that don't trigger a UI dialog. **(3) Behavioural contract.** Production hot path untouched. Existing tests L1–L15 and S1–S15 untouched. The new S16–S25 tests run only when the user opens Diag → DiLink 2 → Run All (or taps a specific row). Every S test self-marks SKIPPED if ADB shell is unreachable (existing `skipIfNoShell` helper). DL3/DL5 are not affected — the new tests live in `DiLink2TestRunner` which is not invoked outside the DL2 panel. **(4) Build.** `versionCode` 191 → 192, `versionName` 1.2.5 → 1.2.6. Touched files outside CHANGELOG/gradle: `DiagActivity.java` (defaultTab branch), `dilink2/DiLink2TestRunner.java` (catalog +10 entries, dispatcher +10 cases, +`DiscoveredPkg` holder, +`sLastDiscovery` list, +`S17_INTERESTING` whitelist, +`s17IsInteresting` helper, +10 `runS16..runS25` test bodies — ~260 LoC). No new permissions, dependencies, drawables, layouts, strings. **(5) Validation plan.** (a) Install on DL2 device. Open DashCast → Diag → **expected: opens directly on the DiLink 2 tab** (no manual swipe needed). (b) Tap `Lancer tous` → wait ~10 s → check 40 rows now appear (L1–L15 + S1–S25). (c) S16 PASS with N pkg discovered. (d) S17 PASS with `N APK(s) in Download/dl2_apks/`. Open BYD file manager → `Download` → `dl2_apks` → confirm `com.byd.cluster_v<X>.apk` is present (first in alphabetical order anyway, but extracted first by S17's sort). (e) `Envoyer (.log)` → ship via Telegram. The S17 detail + S21/S22 manifest dumps + S23 framework listing combined give us the complete RE starting point for DL2 cluster work — drop the cluster APK into jadx and start mapping the projection API. (f) On DL3/DL5 — install, open Diag, confirm default tab is still DiLink 5 (DL5 device) or Beta Engine (DL3 device with neither auto-detection triggered). Manually switch to DiLink 2 tab → tap `Lancer tous` → S16/S17 will discover and extract whichever BYD packages happen to be installed (useful side benefit for cross-platform comparison) but most L tests will WARN with `not a DL2 signature`. |
| **1.2.5-build191** | 191 | **DL2 hard-ban on every display-resize shell command (defense in depth).** Field log of DL2 testeur (22/05/2026, post-build-190) confirmed (a) DL2 has a single physical display 0 (1280×660, no PRESENTATION display, no second framebuffer — L3 / L5 / S4 / S5 / S15 all PASS/WARN as expected), (b) the BYD MTK MT6765 ROM silently re-routes `wm overscan|size|density -d N` to display 0 when display N does not exist (root cause of the screen-shrink incident the user self-recovered by resetting margins). Even though build 190 already hides the Settings → Marges card on DL2 via `applyDiLink2OverscanGuard`, ANY current or future caller that issues a resize shell command could still reach the ROM and shrink the main UI. Build 191 closes that hole at the lowest possible layer. **(1) `AdbLocalClient.isDiLink2Safe(Context)`** — public mirror of `isDiLink5Safe`, exception-safe wrapper around `Platform.get().isDiLink2(ctx)` that falls back to `false` on any reflection error so the DL3/DL5 hot path is bit-for-bit identical. **(2) `AdbLocalClient.isDisplayResizeCmd(String)`** — regex `(?s).*\bwm\s+(overscan|size|density)\b.*` matches any command line that contains `wm overscan|size|density` (with any arguments, anywhere in the line, including pipelines and `&&` chains). **(3) `AdbLocalClient.blockDiLink2Resize(Context, String)`** — centralised guard returning `true` (with an `AppLogger.w` line `"DL2 BLOCK: refused resize cmd \"…\" — single display 0, would shrink main screen"`) when both predicates fire, `false` otherwise. **(4) Guard wired in `AdbLocalClient.executeShell`** (fire-and-forget) — early-return before the executor dispatch, no callback to honour. **(5) Guard wired in `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult`** — early-return with `callback.onError("blocked on DiLink 2: no cluster display (would shrink main screen)")` so any UI surfacing the error sees a clear message instead of a silent no-op. **(6) Guard wired at the TOP of `ShellGateway.execShellWithResult`** — must be checked BEFORE the proxy-ON path because Beta Engine bypasses `AdbLocalClient.executeShell*` entirely (goes straight to `BetaProxyClient.runShell` / typed verbs); without this layer the typed `setOverscan` path or the proxy `runShell` would let a DL2 resize through. Same error callback contract. **(7) `SettingsActivity.applyOverscan()` — DL2 hard guard at entry.** Defense in depth on top of the build-190 `applyDiLink2OverscanGuard` UI hide: even if the card somehow gets shown (deep-link, future refactor, runtime UI inflation bug), `applyOverscan()` now early-returns when `Platform.get().isDiLink2(this)` with the user-visible message `"⚠️ Marges désactivées sur DiLink 2 (un seul écran)."` in `tv_overscan_result` and an `AppLogger.w` line for forensics. **(8) Coverage matrix.** Every production resize call site is now blocked on DL2 by at least one layer: `SettingsActivity.applyOverscan` (layer 7+2+3+4), `MainActivity.autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` + resize SeekBar (layer 6+2+3 via ShellGateway), `ClusterService.applyClusterFreeformBounds` + `onDashboardDisplayConnected` + `stopProjectionNoAdb` (layer 6+2+3 via ShellGateway). The dead-code `AdbLocalClient.runClusterDisplaySizeTest` (manual diagnostic, no UI caller) bypasses `executeShell*` by going through raw `dadb.shell` — left untouched, would need separate handling if ever wired to a button. **(9) Behavioural contract.** DL3 / DL5 zero behaviour change: `isDiLink2Safe` returns false on both, so `blockDiLink2Resize` is a no-op short-circuit before any work. The `wm overscan -d 1` on DL3 (cluster) and `wm overscan -d 3` on DL5 still run exactly as in build 190. On DL2 every such command is intercepted with a single WARN log line and an `onError` callback when applicable. **(10) Build.** `versionCode` 190 → 191, `versionName` 1.2.4 → 1.2.5. Touched files outside CHANGELOG / gradle: `AdbLocalClient.java` (+`isDiLink2Safe`, +`isDisplayResizeCmd`, +`blockDiLink2Resize`, +2 early-return guards), `beta/ShellGateway.java` (+guard at top of `execShellWithResult`), `SettingsActivity.java` (+DL2 hard guard at entry of `applyOverscan`). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings. **Validation plan.** (a) On DL2 device — install, open Diag → Logs and trigger any code path that would issue `wm overscan/size/density` (impossible from the UI now thanks to build 190 hide, so manual via `am broadcast` or future test): expect log line `DL2 BLOCK: refused resize cmd "<cmd>" — single display 0, would shrink main screen` and no actual command sent. Sanity: install, navigate UI, confirm no spurious `DL2 BLOCK` lines in normal operation (only triggered when something tries a resize). (b) On DL3 — install, open Settings → Marges, slide H=80 V=50, tap Apply: expect `applyOverscan → wm overscan 80,50,80,50 -d 1` log line + cluster screen visible shrinkage (identical to build 190). No `DL2 BLOCK` line should ever appear in DL3 logs. (c) On DL5 — install, open Settings → Marges, tap Apply: expect `applyOverscan → wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 3`, API 32 will reject with `Unknown command: overscan` (acceptable degradation since build 188). |
| **1.2.4-build190** | 190 | **Critical DL2 fix — Settings “Marges” card no longer shrinks the main screen on DiLink 2.** Field report (22/05/2026, DL2 testeur): user changed `Settings → Marges` to H=80 / V=50 and the main BYD UI shrank — *“I changed the margins setting to 80/50 and it made the screen smaller”*. Root cause traced in [SettingsActivity.applyOverscan()](MyBYDApp/app/src/main/java/com/byd/dashcast/SettingsActivity.java): the command was hardcoded `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 1` (= cluster display id on BYD Seal EU DL3). On DL2 there is no display 1 (single display 0, confirmed by `DisplayManager.getDisplays().length == 1` in the L3 / S4 reports of build 189), so the MTK MT6765 ROM silently fell back to applying the overscan to display 0 — the main 1280×660 UI screen the user is looking at. User self-fix was to press the Reset button (which sends `wm overscan 0,0,0,0 -d 1` and accidentally undoes the harm by the same MTK fallback path). **Build 190 hard-fixes the root cause** with the three-layer defense (option C in the 22/05/2026 user decision): **(1) `Platform.detectDiLink2(brand, product, api)` + `Platform.isDiLink2(Context)` + `Platform.isAutoDetectedDiLink2()`.** New auto-detector mirroring `detectDiLink5` but scoped to the alps / k65v1 / API 28-29 signature confirmed by two DL2 field reports (21/05/2026): requires `Build.BRAND == "alps"` AND `ro.product.name` contains `k65` AND `Build.VERSION.SDK_INT` is 28 or 29. Conservative gates — returns false on any uncertainty so the DL3 / DL5 hot path is bit-for-bit identical. Constructor now caches both `autoDiLink5` and `autoDiLink2` at process start; the singleton is built in `DashCastApp.onCreate()` so subsequent calls are zero-cost. **(2) `SettingsActivity.applyDiLink2OverscanGuard()`.** Called in `onCreate()` right after `bindViews / loadPreferences / wireListeners / wireSettingsNavRail`. Hides BOTH the `tv_overscan_section_title` TextView and the `card_overscan` MaterialCardView (`View.GONE`) when EITHER (a) `Platform.isDiLink2(this)` is true OR (b) `resolveClusterDisplayId() < 0` (no secondary display present at activity launch, covers a DL3 / DL5 device where the cluster is physically disconnected). The user simply cannot reach the offending button on DL2 anymore. AppLogger.i records the reason (`DL2 platform detected (alps/k65v1) — no cluster display` or `no secondary display present at launch`) for forensic traceability when investigating future reports. **(3) `SettingsActivity.applyOverscan()` — hardcoded `-d 1` removed.** Now calls `resolveClusterDisplayId()` to obtain the actual cluster display id dynamically; aborts cleanly with `⚠️ Aucun cluster détecté — marges ignorées (DL2 ?).` shown in `tv_overscan_result` if `< 0` is returned. Belt-and-suspenders for any code path that could bypass the guard in (2) (e.g. user-fired intent, deep-link, or a future refactor that re-shows the card). The shell command now reads `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d <resolved>` instead of `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 1`. **(4) `SettingsActivity.resolveClusterDisplayId()`.** Pure java helper: tries `DisplayManager.getDisplays(DISPLAY_CATEGORY_PRESENTATION)` first (the strict cluster filter used by `ClusterManager.findClusterDisplay`), falls back to `getDisplays()` and returns the first non-default id. Returns `-1` on (a) no DisplayManager service available (defensive), (b) no display with id != 0 found (DL2 case), (c) any Throwable (defensive). Logic is identical to the existing `ClusterManager.findClusterDisplay()` helper but with `getDisplayId()` returned directly to avoid an unused `Display` object allocation in the Settings hot path. **(5) Layout `activity_settings.xml`.** Two id additions — `android:id="@+id/tv_overscan_section_title"` on the section header TextView and `android:id="@+id/card_overscan"` on the MaterialCardView — needed for the `View.GONE` toggle in (2). No structural change, no style change, no string change. **(6) Behavioural contract.** On DL3 (BYD Seal EU, the user's main vehicle): `Platform.isDiLink2` returns false, `resolveClusterDisplayId()` returns 1 (the cluster), the card is shown, `applyOverscan` issues `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 1` exactly as before — zero behaviour change. On DL5 (BYD-AUTO testeur): `Platform.isDiLink2` returns false, `resolveClusterDisplayId()` returns 3 (the first `XDJAScreenProjection_*` display), the card is shown, `applyOverscan` now issues `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 3` instead of the previously useless `-d 1` (which on DL5 was rejected by API 30+ anyway with `Unknown command: overscan`, see build 188). DL5 owners can now actually use the margins UI for their cluster — incidental fix. On DL2 (alps / k65v1 / API 28): `Platform.isDiLink2` returns true → the card is `View.GONE` from `onCreate` → the user cannot press Apply → the main screen can never be shrunk by this code path again. **(7) Build.** `versionCode` 189 → 190, `versionName` 1.2.3 → 1.2.4. Touched files: `MyBYDApp/app/build.gradle` (versionCode + versionName), `MyBYDApp/CHANGELOG.md` (this entry), `MyBYDApp/app/src/main/java/com/byd/dashcast/platform/Platform.java` (+`autoDiLink2` field, +`detectDiLink2`, +`isAutoDetectedDiLink2`, +`isDiLink2`), `MyBYDApp/app/src/main/java/com/byd/dashcast/SettingsActivity.java` (+2 view fields, +`applyDiLink2OverscanGuard()`, +`resolveClusterDisplayId()`, `applyOverscan()` rewritten, +2 imports for `DisplayManager`/`Display`/`Platform`), `MyBYDApp/app/src/main/res/layout/activity_settings.xml` (+2 ids). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings (the inline `⚠️ Aucun cluster détecté — marges ignorées (DL2 ?).` is a one-shot diagnostic, the French-only audience for this branch is acceptable). No production hot-path changes outside SettingsActivity. **Validation plan.** (a) On DL2 device — install the OTA, open DashCast → Settings, scroll down, confirm the entire “Marges Overscan” section (title + slider card + Apply/Reset buttons) is no longer visible. Logcat tag `SettingsActivity` should show `Overscan section hidden: DL2 platform detected (alps/k65v1) — no cluster display` at activity start. The previously broken Apply path cannot be triggered. (b) On DL3 Seal EU (production reference device) — install, open Settings, confirm the Marges card IS visible and looks identical to build 189, slide H to 80 / V to 50, tap Apply, confirm the cluster screen shrinks as before, tap Reset, confirm the cluster screen restores. Logcat tag `SettingsActivity` should show `applyOverscan → wm overscan 80,50,80,50 -d 1` (same as build 189) and the result TextView should display `✅ Marges appliquées: H=80px V=50px`. (c) On DL5 testeur — install, open Settings, confirm the Marges card IS visible, slide H to 80 / V to 50, tap Apply, expected log line is `applyOverscan → wm overscan 80,50,80,50 -d 3` (was `-d 1` and silently failing in build 189). On API 32 the `wm overscan` subcommand is removed, so the shell will return `Unknown command: overscan` and the result TextView will display `❌ Unknown command: overscan` — acceptable degradation, no functional regression vs build 189, and a follow-up build can replace the shell call with `BetaProxyClient.setOverscan(displayId, l, t, r, b)` via the typed daemon (already implemented since build 174 for Phase 4a) if needed. (d) On any device where the cluster is physically disconnected at activity launch — the card should be hidden with logcat reason `no secondary display present at launch`. |
| **1.2.3-build189** | 189 | **DiLink 2 diag — 15 new shell-based tests (S1–S15) now that ADB-over-TCP is open on the DL2 testeur device.** Build 185 introduced the DL2 panel with a 15-test reflection-only suite (L1–L15) because ADB-TCP was confirmed CLOSED on the field DL2 device. On 22/05/2026 the user opened ADB on that device, which unlocks the entire shell-driven diagnostic surface that DL3 / DL5 already enjoy. Build 189 adds a second test tier (S1–S15) that complements the existing recon tests with active shell probes via the existing `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` relay (uid 2000, dadb dispatcher). The new tier is additive — every S test self-marks `SKIPPED` if the shell is unreachable (returns `__SHELL_ERR__:…` internally), so legacy DL2 devices with ADB closed still see the panel as fully green on the L tier and cleanly skipped on the S tier. **(1) `dilink2/DiLink2TestRunner.java` — new sync shell helper.** `runShellSync(Context, String, long timeoutMs)` wraps the existing async `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` in a `CountDownLatch` + `AtomicReference<String>` so the dilink2-test-runner thread can call shell synchronously without owning its own background executor (same pattern as `BetaTestRunner.testX1/X2`). Errors and dispatch exceptions are funneled into a sentinel string `__SHELL_ERR__:<reason>`; `isShellErr` / `skipIfNoShell` helpers let every S test add a 1-line gate at the top. `safeTrim` caps detail output at a configurable max with an explicit truncation marker. **(2) Catalog +15 entries S1–S15** appended after L15 in `catalog()`. **(3) Dispatcher** — `runAll()` switch extended with 15 new `case "Sn"` lines mapping to `runSn(ctx, r)`. **(4) Test bodies (15 new private static methods, ~270 LoC).** **S1** — ADB shell smoke: `id -u && id -un && pwd && uname -a` — PASS if first line is a numeric uid (typically 2000=shell). **S2** — `getprop` filtered on `byd|alps|adb|cluster|display|sf.lcd` — PASS if any property matched, useful to compare DL2 properties against the dump we already have. **S3** — `wm size && wm density && wm overscan` — CRITICAL test that directly addresses the field-reported issue "applications plus petites que l'écran" after running Diag tests: detects `Override:` lines and non-zero overscan tuples; WARN with an explicit "`wm size reset && wm overscan reset` to restore" message when display state is overridden. **S4** — `dumpsys display` filtered on overscan/Override/Physical/state — captures per-display geometry block count. **S5** — `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger --display-id` + HWC display lines — physical compositor inventory. **S6** — `service list` filtered on the 11 cluster/projection-related keywords — visible binder service inventory from the shell perspective (which can differ from what the app uid sees, useful to spot SELinux-shielded services). **S7** — `service call AutoContainer 1` + `service call auto_container 1` — direct probe of BYD's cluster bridge service on DL2 (expected absent — Phase 4 SDK is DL3+). **S8** — `pm list packages -f` filtered + sorted — full APK path inventory for BYD/Alps/Xdja/cluster packages, foundation for any future D10-style APK extraction on DL2. **S9** — `dumpsys activity activities` filtered — current resumed activity + display routing snapshot. **S10** — `getenforce && id -Z && ls -Zd /system/bin/sh` — SELinux enforcing state + SELinux domain of the shell process. **S11** — `ps -A -o PID,USER,NAME` filtered on cluster/projection/byd daemon names (with `ps`-only fallback for toyboxes that reject `-A`). **S12** — `settings list system|secure|global` filtered on byd/cluster/display/overscan keywords — captures any user-settable BYD overrides. **S13** — `am start --display 0` + `am start --display 1` on the DashCast launcher activity itself — probes whether DL2 even exposes display id 1 to `am start`; the response disambiguates "no second display" from "second display present but uid-protected". **S14** — `/proc/cpuinfo` (Hardware/Processor lines) + `/proc/meminfo` head + `df -h /data` — hardware fingerprint cross-check (expected `MT67xx` for the MTK MT6765 SoC). **S15** — Filesystem inventory of `/sys/class/drm/`, `/dev/graphics/`, `/vendor/etc/byd/`, `/system/etc/byd/`, `/data/byd/` — direct DRM + framebuffer + BYD config dir listing, ground-truth where the BYD-specific files actually live (or don't) on this MTK ROM. **(5) Behavioural contract.** Production hot path untouched. The L tier (L1–L15) is bit-for-bit unchanged. DL3 / DL5 code paths untouched. The S tier shares zero state with the L tier and gracefully skips if `AdbLocalClient` rejects the shell connection (legacy DL2 with closed ADB). Each S test has a per-command timeout (4–8 s depending on expected workload) so a hung shell never blocks the suite. **(6) Build.** `versionCode` 188 → 189, `versionName` 1.2.2 → 1.2.3. Sole touched file outside CHANGELOG / gradle: `dilink2/DiLink2TestRunner.java` (doc comment refresh + catalog +15 entries + dispatcher +15 cases + `runShellSync` helper + 15 new `runSn` methods + `isShellErr` / `skipIfNoShell` / `safeTrim` helpers). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings (S tests reuse the existing `item_beta_test.xml` row + DiagActivity DL2 panel which iterates `catalog()` programmatically), no new resources. **Validation plan.** (a) Install on the DL2 device (ADB now open). Open Diag → DiLink 2 tab → tap `Lancer tous`. (b) Expected: 30 rows now appear (L1–L15 + S1–S15) instead of 15. L1–L15 results identical to build 188. S1 should PASS with `uid=2000`; S3 is the focus — if WARN, the user has the exact reset command to type; if PASS, the field-reported "apps smaller than screen" symptom is unrelated to display geometry and we look elsewhere. (c) `Envoyer (.log)` → share the full report via Telegram. The S6 + S7 + S8 outputs combined will tell us if any BYD cluster service exists on DL2 that's invisible to the app uid (= explains why L7 reports nothing despite the cluster screen physically existing). (d) On any DL2 device with ADB still closed (legacy field units), expected: L1–L15 PASS as before, S1–S15 all SKIPPED with message "ADB shell unreachable: …" — non-destructive degradation. (e) On DL3 / DL5, the DL2 tab is still visible (we don't gate it behind Platform.isDiLink2 because Platform doesn't have that detector yet); S1–S15 will all PASS with data that's useful for nothing on those platforms — UI inconvenience only, no production risk. |
| **1.2.2-build188** | 188 | **DiLink 5 app launch via ADB shell + `wm overscan` gated on DL3.** Field log `byd_report_20260522_132030.log` (build 187) showed the new DL5 projection wiring works perfectly up to display readiness (`DL5 cluster display ready: id=3 name=shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_0`, `Cluster display connected: id=3`, `Cluster dimensions: 1920x720 displayId=3`), then crashes at the next step: `SecurityException: Permission Denial: starting Intent ... with launchDisplayId=3 from com.byd.dashcast (uid=10148)`. Our process uid 10148 is denied cross-display launches by IActivityTaskManager on DL5 even though `INTERNAL_SYSTEM_WINDOW` is granted — this differs from DL3 where the same reflection path works. The D31 diagnostic probe (build 186) had already validated the workaround: route the launch through ADB shell (uid 2000, which has the BYD `auto_container` whitelisting + the unrestricted activity-launch privileges). Build 188 ports this workaround into production. **(1) `AdbLocalClient.isDiLink5Safe(Context)` visibility bumped from `private static` to package-private** so `ClusterService` (same package) can reuse the existing exception-safe DL5 detector instead of duplicating the try/catch around `Platform.get().isDiLink5(ctx)`. No behaviour change. **(2) New `ClusterService.startActivityViaShell(packageName, displayId, launchIntent)`.** Resolves the launcher activity from `launchIntent.getComponent()` (which `getPackageManager().getLaunchIntentForPackage(pkg)` already populates), builds `am start --display N -a android.intent.action.MAIN -c android.intent.category.LAUNCHER -n <pkg>/<launcher> --activity-clear-task 2>&1`, and dispatches via `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` so the existing dadb-based shell relay (with cached ADB key pair) runs on the background executor. Errors are logged via `AppLogger.e` but not propagated to the `LaunchCallback` — consistent with the legacy IATM path which also reports `onResult(true)` after best-effort dispatch (RemoteException paths are swallowed there too). Command pattern is bit-identical to D31, the diagnostic probe that PASSed end-to-end on the DL5 testeur on 22/05/2026. **(3) `launchOnDashboard()` — DL5 branch.** Right before the legacy `startActivityViaIAM(launchIntent, opts)` call, the method now tests `AdbLocalClient.isDiLink5Safe(this)`: on DL5 it routes to `startActivityViaShell(packageName, displayId, launchIntent)` instead. The `applyClusterFreeformBounds()` call still runs upstream and still applies `setLaunchWindowingMode(FREEFORM)` + `setLaunchBounds(...)` to the ActivityOptions — these are inert on DL5 (the shell `am start` ignores ActivityOptions entirely) but cost nothing to compute, and they are needed for the DL3 path which falls through to `startActivityViaIAM` unchanged. **(4) `launchOnDashboardWithBounds()` — DL5 branch.** Same pattern as (3): on DL5 the shell path takes over, on DL3 the typed IATM path runs unchanged. No new dependencies, no new permissions, no protocol changes, no new resources, no new strings. **Validation plan.** Push to the DL5 testeur device, install, reboot. | **1.2.0-build186** | 186 | **DiLink 5 diag — D10 extraction relocated to user-visible Download/ + new D31 end-to-end cluster projection probe.** Two scope items on top of build 185, both rooted in the 22/05/2026 DL5 field log (`byd_report_20260522_115956.log`) and the user's live testing on the DL5 vehicle. **(1) D10 — APK extraction now lands in `/storage/emulated/0/Download/dashcast_apks/` instead of the app-private `getExternalFilesDir(null)/extracted_apks/`.** Field-tester feedback: the previous output dir was effectively invisible — the BYD file manager doesn't surface app-private external dirs without root, so the user could see "PASS, 7 APKs ready" in the report but had no way to actually grab the files without `adb pull` from a PC. Worse, every re-run added duplicate-or-cached copies into a dir the user could never empty by hand, slowly eating internal storage build after build. Fix: all filesystem ops in `runD10` now go through the ADB shell (uid 2000, which on Android 10+ has unrestricted write access to `/sdcard/Download/`) — `mkdir -p` for the new dir, `stat -c %s` for the cache check (avoids relying on the app uid being able to `read()` the destination), `cat <src> > <dst> && stat -c %s <dst>` for the copy. At start-up, `runD10` also `rm -rf`'s the legacy `getExternalFilesDir(null)/extracted_apks/` so the next install no longer accumulates dead bytes. The PASS message now reads `N APK(s) in Download/dashcast_apks/ (X cached), Y skipped`. The destination is browsable by the stock BYD file manager (visible under `Download` → `dashcast_apks`), which removes the `adb pull` requirement entirely for users without a PC. **(2) D31 — Live cluster projection end-to-end probe.** First test that drives the full visual cycle the user has been doing by hand to confirm DL5 projection works: `sendInfo(1000, 16)` (opens the projection tunnel, the `shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_*` layerStack becomes visible — initially black, this is what the user confirmed seeing during D28 of build 185) → sleep 1.5 s → `am start --display N -n <selected pkg>/<launcher activity>` (pushes a real activity onto the projection layerStack so the cluster shows the app, not just a black window) → sleep 3 s (user observes the cluster) → `dumpsys activity activities` snapshot filtered on the package and `displayId=` / `topResumed` to correlate the visual with the stack state → `am force-stop <pkg>` → `sendInfo(1000, 18)` (close projection) → `sendInfo(1000, 0)` (restore native video). Loops once per PRESENTATION display (the DL5 device exposes #3 and #4 as `XDJAScreenProjection_0/1`). **(2a) Target-package selection.** D31 deliberately reuses the package already chosen for D8 via the dropdown at the top of the DL5 panel — no new UI, no new prefs key. The package must be installed: `runD31` calls `PackageManager.getPackageInfo(pkg, 0)` up front and FAILs with `Package '<pkg>' is not installed on this device — install it first` (plus a detail line pointing the user to either install it or pick another package from the D8 dropdown) before touching the cluster. SKIPS with `No target package selected — pick one in the D8 dropdown above` when D8 was never run. This directly answers the question raised by the user about D31 hardcoding `com.github.standardadb`: D31 does not hardcode anything, it reads the user's selection. **(2b) Launcher resolution.** Reuses the existing helper `resolveLauncherComponent(ctx, pkg)` so `am start --display N -n <ComponentName>` always targets the package's declared LAUNCHER activity, whatever its concrete class name is. Returns FAIL with `Could not resolve a launcher activity for '<pkg>'` if the package declares no launcher (rare — typically a service-only package). **(2c) Status semantics.** PASS = every `am start --display` returned without `error` / `SecurityException` / `Permission Denial` — message reads `Cycle run on N display(s) — user to confirm visual rendering` to keep the final go/no-go in the user's hands (this is a visual test; the shell can only say the API accepted the call, not that pixels actually reached the cluster). WARN on partial success, FAIL if every launch was refused. The full per-display sequence (commands + outputs + activity-stack snapshot) is dumped to `r.detail` so the user can read the report to understand which display did or did not light up. **(3) DL5 test catalog grows from 30 to 31 entries.** The new D31 row is appended after D30 in the runner catalog and inflated as a normal row by the existing `prepareDl5TestRows()` / `bindDl5Row()` UI code — no layout, strings, drawables or new dependencies. **(4) Behavioural contract.** Production hot path (cluster projection lifecycle, `ClusterManager`, `AdbLocalClient`, daemon code) is untouched. The new D31 sequence is opt-in (the user has to open Diag → DiLink 5 → tap D31 or `Run all`). D31 leaves the cluster in a clean state regardless of failure (`am force-stop` + `sendInfo(18)` + `sendInfo(0)` are always issued at the end of each per-display iteration, including on partial failure). **(5) Build.** `versionCode` 185 → 186, `versionName` pinned `"1.2.0"`. Sole touched files outside CHANGELOG / gradle: `dilink5/DiLink5TestRunner.java` (D10 body rewritten to shell-based ops + legacy dir cleanup, D31 added at the end of the file as a private static method, catalog +1 entry, dispatcher +1 case). |
| **1.2.0-build185** | 185 | **New DiLink 2 recon diagnostic tab — reflection-only test suite (L1–L15) for the alps / k65v1_64_bsp / MT6765 / Android 9 platform.** Two DL2 field reports (`byd_log_20260521_220933.log` and `byd_report_20260521_222022.log`, 327 lines of SysInfo dump) confirmed the DL2 environment differs from DL3/DL5 on every dimension that matters for projection: **(a)** brand=`alps`, manufacturer=`alps`, product=`full_k65v1_64_bsp`, hardware=`mt6765`, API=28, `ro.byd.*` properties ALL undefined — no BYD identity exposed via system properties; **(b)** ADB-over-TCP `127.0.0.1:5555` is CLOSED (`ECONNREFUSED` confirmed twice in the log, both at boot for `SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW` auto-grant and on the `executeShellWithResult "echo ok"` smoke test) — our entire `AdbLocalClient` shell stack is unreachable on DL2; **(c)** `DisplayManager` exposes a single display (Built-in Screen 1280×660 @160dpi, no PRESENTATION-typed display) even though the vehicle physically has a digital cluster screen — the cluster output is not surfaced to the Android display framework; **(d)** `android.app.ActivityTaskManager` is absent (IATM was introduced in API 29, this device is API 28) — every current reflection path that targets IATM throws ClassNotFoundException; **(e)** no XDJA / Auto_container / containerservice / clusterdebug / crosscontrol / windowmanagement / smarttravel / magicwindow package is installed — the BYD ecosystem we built DL3/DL5 support on top of does not exist on this platform; only `com.byd.cluster v1.0` and `com.byd.appstartmanagement v1.0` are present (vs v1.5.0.0 of appstartmanagement on DL5). **Positive surface confirmed in the log:** `SurfaceControl` accessible after `unlockHiddenApis`, `ActivityOptions.setLaunchDisplayId` available, 3/5 BYDAuto*Device SDK classes loadable via reflection (Speed / Energy / Gearbox). **Design choice for build 185.** Because the shell stack is dead on DL2, the new test runner is built on a completely different foundation than DL5: **zero shell calls**, every probe uses Java APIs callable from the app process — `SystemProperties.get` via reflection, `ServiceManager.listServices/getService` via reflection, `PackageManager` queries, `DisplayManager` + reflective `DisplayManagerGlobal.getDisplayIds`, `IActivityManager` method enumeration via reflection, raw `Socket` TCP probes on localhost ADB ports, and `/proc` + `/sys` scans via `java.io.File`. This means the suite runs and produces useful data even when ADB-TCP is unreachable. **(1) `dilink2/DiLink2TestRunner.java` (new file, 15 tests, ~580 LoC).** Self-contained: own `Status` enum, own `TestDef` / `TestResult` / `Listener` types, single-thread executor named `dilink2-test-runner`, ordered catalog, dispatcher. **L1 — Platform fingerprint.** Dumps the full `Build.*` triplet (BRAND / MANUFACTURER / MODEL / PRODUCT / DEVICE / HARDWARE / BOARD / DISPLAY / FINGERPRINT / TAGS / SDK_INT / RELEASE) plus 26 selected `ro.*`, `persist.sys.*`, `sys.*` and `service.adb.tcp.port` properties read via reflected `SystemProperties.get`. PASS when `Build.BRAND=alps` AND `Build.PRODUCT` contains `k65v1` (DL2 signature), WARN otherwise (so the runner is informative on DL3/DL5 too without breaking them). **L2 — ADB local TCP ports probe.** Pure-Java `Socket.connect(InetSocketAddress, 200ms)` on `127.0.0.1` ports 5037 / 5554 / 5555 / 5556 / 4444, with timeout-bucketing of error classification (OPEN vs CLOSED vs TIMEOUT vs ERR). WARN when 0 ports open (expected on DL2 — confirms the shell-disabled environment), PASS when any port responds. **L3 — Multi-display reflective scan.** `DisplayManager.getDisplays()` + `DisplayManager.getDisplays(DISPLAY_CATEGORY_PRESENTATION)` cross-checked against reflective `DisplayManagerGlobal.getDisplayIds()` — the latter returns the raw list before category filtering and sometimes surfaces ids hidden from the standard enumeration. **L4 — SurfaceFlinger physical displays via IBinder.** `ServiceManager.getService("SurfaceFlinger")` + `IBinder.getInterfaceDescriptor()` — deliberately does NOT call `transact()` with custom codes because the SurfaceFlinger transaction layout has been reshuffled across AOSP forks and a wrong code on a vendor binder can SIGSEGV the calling process. PASS on binder reachability alone. **L5 — DRM / framebuffer inventory.** Lists `/sys/class/drm`, `/dev/graphics`, `/sys/class/graphics` via `java.io.File.listFiles()`; PASS when fb1/fb2 OR multiple `card0-*` connectors are visible (= a second hardware display exists outside Android's view), WARN on single framebuffer. **L6 — ServiceManager full inventory + filter.** Reflective `ServiceManager.listServices()` (returns a `String[]` of every registered binder service) then regex-filtered on `cluster|display|secondary|byd|alps|auto|mirror|magic|window|fission|xdja|projection|cross`. Each match prefixed with `  ` for the report. **L7 — Cluster service candidates brute probe.** `ServiceManager.getService()` on 33 hand-curated candidate names spanning DL3/DL5 conventions (`cluster`, `byd_cluster`, `Auto_container`, `auto_container`), MTK conventions (`mtk_cluster`, `mtk_displayfeature`), alps conventions (`alps_cluster`, `alps_display`) and generic suspects (`secondary_display`, `displayfeature`, `mirror`, `magicwindow`, `crosscontrol`, `xdja_container`, `byd_carapi`, `BYDMgmt`, `byd_datacached`, `IBYDCDRService`, `projection`, `carprojection`, `fission`, `appstartmanagement`, etc.); for each PRESENT binder also dumps `IBinder.getInterfaceDescriptor()` so we know the actual AIDL interface name. **L8 — IActivityManager method enumeration.** Reflection on `android.app.IActivityManager` (NOT `IActivityTaskManager` since DL2 is API 28) filtered on `movetask|setlaunch|startactivity|moveactivitytask|settaskwindowing|getfocusedstack|attachapplication` — each entry shows the full method signature so we can build the right typed invocation path. **L9 — ActivityOptions.setLaunchDisplayId smoke.** Builds `ActivityOptions.makeBasic()`, invokes `setLaunchDisplayId(0)` reflectively, then `toBundle()` to confirm the bundled keys are populated (PASS expected, the SysInfo dump already showed this method is present on DL2). **L10 — BYD packages dynamic scan.** `PackageManager.getInstalledPackages(0)` filtered on `com.byd.*` / `com.alps.*` / `com.xdja.*` / `*cluster*` / `*dilink*` — captures package name + versionName + versionCode + APK path with zero shell. **L11/L12 — Manifest deep dive on `com.byd.cluster` and `com.byd.appstartmanagement`** (the only two BYD packages of interest installed on DL2): `getPackageInfo(GET_ACTIVITIES|GET_SERVICES|GET_RECEIVERS|GET_PROVIDERS|GET_PERMISSIONS)` produces a structured dump of every declared component with exported flag, all declared permissions with protection level, and all requested permissions — lets us see exactly what these packages can do and what entry points they expose. SKIPPED gracefully when the package is not installed. **L13 — BYD SDK classpath probe.** `Class.forName` + reflective `getInstance()` on 8 `BYDAuto*Device` classes (Speed / Energy / Gearbox / AC / AirConditioner / Door / Light / Wiper). Distinguishes "class loadable" from "instance obtainable" — the SysInfo dump showed 3/5 of the original 5 we probed work on DL2; the new probe widens that to 8 classes for a fuller picture. **L14 — `/proc` cluster process scan.** Pure-Java scan of `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` (`new File("/proc").listFiles()`) filtered on `cluster|fission|projection|secondary|surface|display`, capped at 40 matches — reveals which native daemons (e.g. `surfaceflinger`, MTK display daemons, vendor cluster handlers) are visible to the app uid even when the binder facing them isn't. **L15 — Hidden-API reachability sanity.** Reflective `ServiceManager.getService` + `getInterfaceDescriptor()` on 6 core system binders (`window`, `activity`, `package`, `display`, `input`, `power`); PASS when at least 4 are reachable — confirms the app process can still talk to system services despite SELinux on API 28 (relevant because some recon strategies on DL2 will need a typed binder path instead of shell). All exceptions inside the test bodies are caught and reported as `FAIL` with `<ExceptionClass>: <message>`; the dispatcher's `Throwable` catch is a defense-in-depth so a single test crash never breaks the suite. **(2) `res/layout/include_diag_dilink2.xml` (new file).** Mirrors the DL5 panel design but simpler — no D8-style live launch, no app picker, just: a header card with title `Plateforme DiLink 2 — Recon`, dynamic subtitle (`Produit : %1$s · brand : %2$s · API %3$d`), a signature pill (`DL2 détectée` / `non-DL2`), the run/copy counters, two Material buttons `Lancer tous` / `Envoyer (.log)`, and a `LinearLayout ll_dl2_test_list` populated programmatically from `DiLink2TestRunner.catalog()` using the existing `item_beta_test.xml` row layout (full re-use to avoid layout duplication). **(3) `DiagActivity.java` wiring.** New tab constant `TAB_DILINK2 = 7` (Sniffer shifted to 8). New `panelDl2` field bound in `bindTabs()`, `showPanelForTab` now toggles a 5th panel. New `bindDl2Panel()` resolves DL2 signature from `Build.BRAND` + `Build.PRODUCT` at bind time (no Platform changes — we deliberately keep `Platform.java` untouched, the DL2 signature detection is a UI-side hint only). New `prepareDl2TestRows()` / `bindDl2Row()` / `runDl2AllTests()` / `updateDl2Counters()` / `copyDl2Report()` mirror exactly the DL5 lifecycle, with their own `dl2RowViews` / `dl2LastResults` lists so no DL5 / Beta state can collide. The copy-report path goes through `AppLogger.shareWithReport()` to ship as `.log` attachment. **(4) `activity_diag.xml` — new `<TabItem android:text="@string/diag_tab_dilink2"/>` inserted between DiLink 5 and Sniffer + new `<include android:id="@+id/panel_dilink2" layout="@layout/include_diag_dilink2"/>` inside `fl_diag_content`. **(5) `strings.xml` — 9 new keys** (`diag_tab_dilink2`, `diag_dl2_header_title`, `diag_dl2_header_subtitle`, `diag_dl2_header_subtitle_fmt`, `diag_dl2_pill_unknown`, `diag_dl2_pill_detected`, `diag_dl2_pill_other`, `diag_dl2_counters_fmt`). French-only for now (the 12 locale files are intentionally NOT updated yet — will batch with the next translation pass once the DL2 panel labels stabilize after field testing). **No production-path changes.** Zero touches to `ClusterManager`, `AdbLocalClient`, `Platform.java`, `BetaTestRunner`, `DiLink5TestRunner`, no new permissions, no new dependencies, no new protocol verbs. DL3 and DL5 behaviour is bit-for-bit identical to build 184. **Build.** `versionCode` 184 → 185, `versionName` pinned `"1.2.0"` per channel policy. **(6) Horizontal swipe navigation in Diag (Option B, lightweight).** With 9 tabs (Cluster / Display / ADB local / Système / ADAS / Beta Engine / DiLink 5 / DiLink 2 / Sniffer) the tab bar is getting long. Build 185 attaches a `GestureDetector.SimpleOnGestureListener` to the `FrameLayout fl_diag_content` (single touch listener, ~40 LoC, no architecture change): a clear horizontal fling (|dx| ≥ 120 px AND |dx| ≥ 1.5×|dy| AND |vx| ≥ 220 px/s) selects the next or previous tab via `tabs.getTabAt(next).select()` which reuses the existing `OnTabSelectedListener` so the same `showPanelForTab` path runs. The listener returns `false` so child scrollers (NestedScrollView in DL5/DL2/Sniffer panels, RecyclerView rows in test lists, vertical scroll inside SysInfo report) keep working untouched — only clear horizontal flings are intercepted. The threshold checks reject diagonal swipes and tap noise. No animation between panels (the bascule is dry, like the current tab click) — a proper `ViewPager2 + FragmentStateAdapter` refactor with animated slide is queued for a later build (see `/memories/repo/byd-project.md` TODO: needs 5 new Fragment classes + refactor of all 5 `bind*Panel` methods + retest on DL3/DL5/DL2). Wiring: new `attachSwipeNavigation(View)` method called once in `bindTabs()`. **Validation plan.** (a) Install on the DL2 testeur device, open Diag, navigate to the new DiLink 2 tab, confirm the signature pill reads `Signature : DL2 détectée`, press `Lancer tous`, wait for the 15-test pass (~3 s total — all tests are sub-second since none of them hit shell). (b) Press `Envoyer (.log)`, share the resulting `byd_report_*.log` via Telegram. (c) Cross-check on a DL3 or DL5 device that the tab is still visible (we did NOT gate it behind Platform.isDiLink2 since we have no DL2 detection in Platform yet) and that pressing `Lancer tous` produces a report where L1 reports WARN ("not a DL2 signature") and the other tests still produce useful data — confirms the suite is non-destructive on non-DL2 platforms. The DL2 report will tell us, for the first time, (i) whether any non-DisplayManager surface exposes a second physical display via SurfaceFlinger or DRM, (ii) which binder services BYD or alps registered for cluster control, (iii) what the `com.byd.cluster` package on DL2 actually contains and exposes, (iv) which `IActivityManager` methods are available for typed display routing on API 28 without IATM, (v) whether the ADB-TCP situation can be unblocked from the device side. Based on these answers, build 186 will design the actual DL2 cluster projection strategy. |
| **1.2.0-build184** | 184 | **Diag UI — new ADAS quick-toggle tab + DiLink 5 test polish based on the build-183 field log.** First DL5 field log (`byd_report_20260522_092905.log`, build 183) gave us actionable signal on three fronts: (a) clusterdebug v1.6.1.4 enumeration confirmed AutoContainer `sendInfo` codes 12 (显示Adas / Show ADAS) and 13 (关闭Adas / Hide ADAS) are universal across all DiLink platforms (DL3 / Di4 / DL5 / DL6) — stripped only on R-series; (b) D28 was running a `sendInfo(30)` screen-size hint that is a no-op on DL5 because the cluster never reads it (the corresponding slot is empty in clusterdebug `_Di5` variant); (c) D10 was re-`cat`ing every whitelisted APK on every run even though the destination filename already embeds `versionCode`. Build 184 turns those three findings into shipped fixes plus a new always-visible quick-toggle for the ADAS overlay. **(1) New `ADAS` diagnostic tab** replaces the previously unused `Stress test` placeholder (tab position #5). Layout `include_diag_adas.xml` (190 lines, 3 M3 cards in a `NestedScrollView`): (a) header card with title, subtitle, a runtime status pill showing the resolved service name (`Auto_container` on DL3/Di4 / `auto_container` on DL5+), and two filled / tonal `MaterialButton`s `Afficher ADAS (12)` and `Masquer ADAS (13)`; (b) result card with a monospace `tv_adas_result` text view showing the last issued command and the raw shell response (Parcel hex or error); (c) info card explaining the underlying `service call <svc> 2 i32 1000 i32 <12|13> s16 ""` invocation, the service-name dispatch rule, and that R-series is unsupported. **(2) Wiring in `DiagActivity.java`**: `TAB_STRESS` (=4) renamed to `TAB_ADAS`, new `panelAdas` field bound in `bindTabs()`, `showPanelForTab` gated on `isAdas`, `TAB_STRESS` removed from the coming-soon switch. New `bindAdasPanel()` resolves the service name once at bind time (visible in the pill) and wires the two buttons to `sendAdasCmd(12)` / `sendAdasCmd(13)`. `sendAdasCmd(int)` re-resolves the service name on every press (so toggling the DL5 override in Settings is picked up without restarting Diag), builds the exact `service call` command line, displays `$ <cmd>\n…envoi en cours…` on press, and writes back the raw daemon response via `safeRunOnUiThread`. Error path prefixes the response with `ERREUR:`. Service dispatch uses `Platform.get().isDiLink5(this)` (try/catch wraps reflection so a future Platform change cannot crash the panel — falls back to the DL3 default `Auto_container`). **(3) `activity_diag.xml`**: 5th `<TabItem>` now references `@string/diag_tab_adas` (new key, value `"ADAS"`, untranslated acronym, added to `values/strings.xml` next to the legacy `diag_tab_stress` which is left in place to keep the 12 locale files unchanged); new `<include android:id="@+id/panel_adas" layout="@layout/include_diag_adas"/>` inserted into `fl_diag_content`. **(4) D10 — APK extraction cache hit.** Filename already embeds `versionCode` (`<pkg>_v<versionCode>.apk`), so if the destination file exists with `length() > 0` we now skip the `cat` redirect entirely, increment a new `cached` counter, and log `↻ <pkg>  (cached, NN KB)`. Message summary reads `N APK(s) ready (X cached), Y skipped — adb pull the dir` instead of the build-183 `N APK(s) extracted`. On a re-run with no new packages, D10 now produces zero shell calls (was N calls per whitelisted package, each up to 15 s of `cat` budget). **(5) D23 rewritten end-to-end.** Build 183 D23 looped over binder transaction codes 0..8 with a neutral payload to enumerate the auto_container API surface — useful one-off but redundant after the clusterdebug enumeration (codes 0/1/2 are interface-transaction / ping / sendInfo respectively, the rest are not transactions but sendInfo *codes*). D23 now sends `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 2 s16 ""` (= sendInfo code 2 = 所有警告灯点亮 / `All warning lamps ON`), waits 3 s via `Thread.sleep(3000)`, then sends `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 3 s16 ""` (= sendInfo code 3 = 所有警告灯熄灭 / `All warning lamps OFF`). Status `WARN` with message asking the user to visually confirm that the warning lamps lit up for ~3 s and went off again; `FAIL` if `service auto_container does not exist`. This is a much stronger test than scanning the binder API: it cross-validates that (a) the snake-case `auto_container` service name reaches the cluster on DL5, (b) the sendInfo path is end-to-end functional and (c) the cluster Qt side honours the published codes from the clusterdebug enumeration. **(6) D28 — dropped the useless `sendInfo(30)` step.** clusterdebug `_Di5` variant has an empty slot at code 30 (was `设置屏幕尺寸` / `Set screen size` on DL3 / Di4, dropped on DL5). The screen-size hint is a no-op on this platform. New D28 flow: `logcat -c` → `sendInfo(16)` (Qt standby ON, real projection trigger) → sleep 3 s → `dumpsys display` → logcat filtered on `auto_container | AutoContainer | cluster | projection | fission | XDJA | magicwindow | MagicWindow | containerservice` (last 80 lines) → cleanup `sendInfo(18)` (close) + `sendInfo(0)` (refresh). Catalog description updated to match. The test now exercises only the real projection trigger, no padding. **(7) Documentation.** Added `doc_api/APK_com.byd.clusterdebug_CODES.md`: full enumeration of clusterdebug v1.6.1.4 (versionCode 10601004) sendInfo codes across all three `infoListInit*` variants (`generic` for DL3/Di4, `_Di5` for DL5, `_R` for R-series), with side-by-side zh / EN translations for every populated slot. Used as the canonical reference for both the ADAS panel codes (12/13) and the D23 warning-lamp cycle (2/3), and as the matrix to audit which AutoContainer codes are platform-stable vs platform-stripped. **(8) Behavioural contract.** Production hot path (`ClusterManager.sendActivationSequence` and the rest of the cluster lifecycle) is unchanged. The ADAS panel reuses the existing `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` background path — no new thread, no new permission, no new system Context. The panel is read-only / write-only with respect to projection state: pressing `Afficher ADAS` while a projection is running just overlays the warning lamps; pressing `Masquer ADAS` does not affect projection. **(9) Build.** `versionCode` 183 → 184, `versionName` pinned `"1.2.0"` per channel policy. Touched files outside CHANGELOG / gradle: `DiagActivity.java` (TAB rename + `panelAdas` field + `bindAdasPanel` + `sendAdasCmd` + `autoContainerSvcName` helper), `dilink5/DiLink5TestRunner.java` (D10 cache logic + D23 full rewrite + D28 sendInfo(30) drop + catalog text for D23 and D28), `res/layout/activity_diag.xml` (tab text + new include), `res/layout/include_diag_adas.xml` (new 190 lines), `res/values/strings.xml` (added `diag_tab_adas="ADAS"`). No new dependencies, drawables (reuses `ic_stethoscope`), permissions, or protocol verbs. **(10) Validation plan.** (a) Install on a DL3 device, open Diag → ADAS tab → confirm pill reads `Auto_container`, press `Afficher ADAS (12)` → expect warning lamps lit on cluster + Parcel response in the result card, press `Masquer ADAS (13)` → expect lamps off. (b) Install on a DL5 device, confirm pill reads `auto_container` (snake_case), repeat the cycle, then run Diag → DiLink 5 → D23 → confirm WARN status with the visual-cycle message, run D10 twice in a row → second pass shows all rows as `cached`, run D28 → confirm the report no longer contains a `sendInfo(30)` section. (c) Toggle the DL5 override in Settings on a DL3 device → reopen Diag → ADAS tab → confirm pill switches between `Auto_container` and `auto_container` and that subsequent button presses use the corresponding service name in the displayed `$` command. |

| **1.2.0-build182** | 182 | **DiLink 5 enablement — prerelease branch (`beta/1.2.0-dilink5`).** Major version bump because DL5 is a different platform (Android 12 / API 32, BYD-AUTO brand, new `com.byd.containerservice` rebrand, new `com.byd.appstartmanagement` gatekeeper) and the diagnostic surface required to triage it is non-trivial. **DiLink 5 diag — 11 new tests (D10–D20) + D9 dynamic discovery + APK extraction (whitelisted) for reverse-engineering.** First DL5 field log (`byd_report_20260521_212356.log`, API 32, BYD-AUTO) revealed three actionable insights: (1) `auto_container [android.os.IAutoContainer]` is still present on DL5 — our typed `Phase4Verbs.autoContainerSendInfo` path may work as-is; (2) display naming is the same `shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_N` family as DL3; (3) the new gatekeepers `com.byd.appstartmanagement 1.5.0.0` + `com.byd.providers.appstartup 12` + the BYD-rebranded `com.byd.containerservice 12` (replacing `com.xdja.containerservice`) are the suspected behavioural divergence. Build 182 expands the DL5 diagnostic suite to triage all three threads. **(1) D9 reworked — dynamic discovery.** Replaces the 7-package hardcoded whitelist with a shell scan: `pm list packages -f \| grep -iE 'com\.byd\.\|com\.xdja\.\|com\.dilink\.\|cluster\|automotive' \| sort -u`. Parses every line (format `package:/data/app/.../base.apk=<pkg>`), reads `versionName` + `getLongVersionCode()` (API 28+) via PackageManager, and stores the result in a static `sLastDiscovery` list of `DiscoveredPkg` records for downstream tests. Output now shows the full APK path on disk for each match. Status WARN if zero matches, PASS otherwise. **(2) D10 — APK extraction for reverse-engineering.** Iterates `sLastDiscovery`, `cat`s each `apkPath` from `/data/app/...` to `getExternalFilesDir(null)/extracted_apks/<pkg>_v<versionCode>.apk` via the existing `AdbLocalClient` shell relay (uid 2000 has read access to world-readable APK files; `cat` redirect avoids `cp` permission quirks observed on some BYD builds). On success, the per-file `ls -l` is logged plus file size in KB. Final report card shows the target dir path with the exact `adb pull` command to retrieve everything in one shot. SKIPPED if D9 was not run first (empty discovery). **(3) D11 — AutoContainer sendInfo refresh probe.** `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 0 s16 ""` (the harmless `sendInfo(0)` refresh that's already part of our restoreBydOnCluster sequence). Pattern-matches the output for `SecurityException` / `Permission Denial` / `Result:` / `Parcel`. PASS = service accepted from uid 2000 (confirms our typed Phase 4c path will work on DL5 without modification). FAIL = SecurityException or service-not-found. **(4) D12 — Qt standby cycle probe.** Active 3-step sequence `sendInfo(16)` (Qt standby) → sleep 1.5s → `sendInfo(18)` (close) → sleep 0.5s → `sendInfo(0)` (refresh). Validates the *protocol cycle*, not just the binder reachability. No projection actually started — these three commands together form a complete null cycle. PASS if no SecurityException / no service-not-found anywhere in the 3-step output. **(5) D13 — IATM.setTaskWindowingMode reflection probe.** Resolves `android.app.ActivityTaskManager.getService()` then reflective `setTaskWindowingMode(0, 4, true)` (taskId=0 = bogus on purpose). Three outcomes: `InvocationTargetException(SecurityException)` → WARN (expected on un-signed app, confirms we need signature perms or proxy daemon path); `InvocationTargetException(IllegalArgumentException)` → PASS (method is reachable, only rejected the bogus taskId — best case, means the API is usable once we have a real taskId); `NoSuchMethodException` → FAIL (method removed). Disambiguates the D6 FAIL line. **(6) D14 — Window manager state dump.** `dumpsys window \| grep -E 'Display #\|mDisplayId\|imeLayerStack\|focused\|mStackId\|mCurrentFocus\|mDisplayContent' \| head -80`. Captures the live windowing state without flooding the report. Useful as a baseline snapshot before reproducing a cluster bug. **(7) D15 — `appstartmanagement` package inspection.** `dumpsys package com.byd.appstartmanagement` filtered to permission/receiver/service/activity/authority/provider/signature/version lines, capped at 120. This is the suspected DL5 launch gatekeeper — the inspection will show which intents/categories/actions it claims and which protectionLevel its custom permissions use. **(8) D16 — `containerservice` package inspection.** Same filter applied to `com.byd.containerservice` (the BYD-rebranded XDJA). Will reveal whether the wake/release transaction codes are the same as the old `com.xdja.containerservice` we have in `decompiled/`. **(9) D17 — proxy daemon spawn capability.** Three-step shell probe: `id` (confirm shell uid) → `which app_process64 app_process` (locate the spawn target) → `app_process64 -Xnoimage-dex2oat /system/bin --nice-name=dl5_probe -version` (actually try to spawn it with our daemon's command flags, capturing the first 5 lines of output) → `ls /system/bin/app_process64`. PASS = app_process64 reachable with no permission denial / no `avc:` (SELinux) lines — our Beta proxy daemon strategy is viable on DL5/API 32. FAIL = SELinux denied. WARN = binary not at expected path. **(10) D18 — SELinux context.** `id` + `ps -Z -o LABEL,USER,PID,NAME \| head -10`. Gives the SELinux domain of the shell user and the first 10 processes, needed to diagnose SELinux-related daemon spawn failures. **(11) D19 — Full system services inventory.** Broader than D3: `service list \| grep -iE 'auto\|byd\|cluster\|fission\|display\|window\|projection\|crosscontrol\|xdja\|task' \| head -60`. Captures the full landscape of cluster-relevant binders — useful for spotting BYD-specific services we don't know about yet. **(12) D20 — IActivityTaskManager service presence.** `service list \| grep -iE 'activity_task\|activitytask'`. Confirms the binder exists at the OS level (required precondition for the D13 typed path). **(13) Discovery handoff between tests.** D9 stores its results in `private static final List<DiscoveredPkg> sLastDiscovery` (cleared at the start of each D9 run, so re-running just the suite gives a fresh capture). D10 consumes it and SKIPs if empty. This is the cleanest cross-test data flow without polluting the `TestResult` data class. **(14) Behavioural contract preserved.** No public API changed; existing tests D1–D8 untouched. The `D8Params` holder, the listener contract, and the report format are unchanged. D10's extraction directory is `Context.getExternalFilesDir(null)/extracted_apks/` — falls back to internal `filesDir` if external is unavailable. All new tests use the existing `runShellSync` helper with appropriate timeouts (4–8 s for inspection, 15 s for D10's APK copy loop). **(15) UI / panel.** No change required — the DL5 panel already iterates `DiLink5TestRunner.catalog()` programmatically and inflates one row per `TestDef`. The 11 new entries surface automatically as new rows in the existing scrollable list. **(16) Build.** `versionCode` 181 → 182, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"`. Touched files: `app/build.gradle` (versionCode), `app/src/main/java/com/byd/dashcast/dilink5/DiLink5TestRunner.java` (catalog 9 → 20 entries, switch extended, runD9 rewritten, 11 new run methods + `DiscoveredPkg` holder — +~270 LoC). No new strings, layouts, drawables, dependencies. **(17) Validation plan.** Push to a DL5 testeur, ask them to (a) open Diag → DiLink 5 tab → tap `Run all`, (b) capture the report via the `Copy` button — expected: PASS on D9 with 10–20 packages discovered, PASS on D10 with all APKs in extracted_apks/, PASS or WARN on D11/D12 (the key signal for whether our typed sendInfo path works), useful detail on D13–D20 for triage; (c) `adb pull /sdcard/Android/data/com.byd.dashcast/files/extracted_apks/` from a PC to retrieve all BYD APKs for offline jadx work. |
| **1.1.9-build181** | 181 | **DiagActivity — restore RE Sniffer as a dedicated Material 3 tab.** The Sniffer system (continuous `logcat` + periodic `dumpsys` snapshots into a single `BYD_RE_Sniffer_*.txt` file on the external files dir) was wiped at v0.9.88 (commit `229059c`) when DiagActivity was refactored into the tabbed layout. Only the file-name reference survived in `AppLogger` (storage-cleanup glob). With new Dilink5 testers sending in field logs, we need the Sniffer back to bound user-reported issues — but plugged into the new M3 tab structure rather than the old stacked sections. **(1) New tab `Sniffer` (position 7).** Added `TAB_SNIFFER = 7` constant in `DiagActivity`, new `<TabItem>` in `activity_diag.xml` after `dilink5`, panel toggled in `showPanelForTab` alongside the existing isBeta/isDl5 branches. **(2) New layout `include_diag_sniffer.xml`.** Three stacked M3 cards inside a `NestedScrollView`, identical chrome to `include_diag_beta_engine.xml` (20 dp corner radius, `md_outline_variant` stroke, `md_surface_container_low` background, 20 dp content padding): (a) **Header/status card** — icon + title + subtitle + status pill (`ACTIF` green / `INACTIF` red) + monospace status line + 4 `MaterialButton`s (`Démarrer` filled, `Arrêter` tonal, `Snapshot` outlined, `Exporter` outlined). (b) **"Contenu capturé" info card** — bullet list explaining what lands in the file (header, live logcat threadtime + events, auto snapshots every 10 s of displays/SF/processes, manual snapshot adds `dumpsys window` + broadcasts history, persistence via setsid). (c) **"Procédure type" info card** — 5-step usage flow ending on "Exporter → Telegram". Labels are hardcoded French with `tools:ignore="HardcodedText"` — power-user diagnostic tool, the 12-locale i18n cost was deemed not worth paying for this surface. **(3) Sniffer state machine restored 1:1 from pre-wipe code (`DiagActivity.java` @1160–1500 at commit `229059c^`).** Constants `RE_SNIFFER_TAG = ".re_sniffer_run"`, `RE_SNIFFER_PIDS = ".re_sniffer_pids"`, `RE_SNIFFER_PREFIX = "BYD_RE_Sniffer_"`, prefs key `byd_diag_prefs / re_sniffer_file_path`. Start path: `logcat -c` + create tag file + write header (date, `getprop ro.product.model`, fingerprint, dumpsys display, dumpsys SurfaceFlinger, ps -A), then `setsid sh -c '…'` × 3 (logcat threadtime, snap loop every 10 s, logcat events buffer) with PIDs captured to `.re_sniffer_pids` for clean teardown. The `setsid` is load-bearing: `nohup` alone doesn't survive adbd `SIGHUP` to the process group. Stop path: removes tag file (snap loop exits at next iteration), reads PID file and `kill -9` each PID, `pkill -f BYD_RE_Sniffer_` fallback. Snapshot path: appends `dumpsys display` + `dumpsys window` + `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger` + `ps -A` + `dumpsys activity broadcasts history` with a `=== SNAPSHOT MANUEL HH:MM:SS ===` separator. Export path: `FileProvider.getUriForFile` + `Intent.ACTION_SEND` with `text/plain` + `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION` + `ClipData.newRawUri` for chooser compatibility on API 29. **(4) Persistence across Activity recreation.** `startSniffer` writes the file path to `SharedPreferences(PREF_SNIFFER)` immediately, `stopSniffer` clears it. New `restoreSnifferState()` runs in `onCreate` after `bindSnifferPanel`: reads the saved path, checks the file exists, then runs `[ -f /data/local/tmp/.re_sniffer_run ] && echo ACTIVE || echo STOPPED` via `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` and restores the UI to ACTIF (green pill, Stop/Snapshot/Export enabled) or "Capture précédente arrêtée — exportable" (Export still enabled because the file is on disk). Covers the case where the user rotates / leaves the activity while a capture is running — they come back and find it still going. **(5) Local helper `safeRunOnUiThread`.** The pre-wipe code used a class-wide `safeRunOnUiThread` that checked `mDestroyed` before delivering. The new DiagActivity didn't have it (Beta Engine panel uses runner-side guards). Added a minimal copy at the bottom of `DiagActivity.java`: skips delivery if `mDestroyed`, also rechecks inside the `runOnUiThread` lambda to cover the race window. Used by all four Sniffer callbacks. **(6) Behavioural contract.** No public API change. No new permissions (`adb_local` shell already has everything the Sniffer needs — it was always running uid 2000 since v0.x). The capture is fully opt-in (button-driven) and survives reboot **only as a stale file** on disk: the tag file lives in `/data/local/tmp` and is wiped on reboot, so the `restoreSnifferState` post-reboot check correctly reports "Capture précédente arrêtée" and clears the prefs. **(7) Wire format unchanged.** No daemon work, no new typed verb — Sniffer uses the legacy `AdbLocalClient.executeShell` / `executeShellWithResult` paths exclusively. The new `AtomicInteger`-based coordinator guarantees finish is called exactly once, on the completion of whichever task finishes last (pre-warm + cleanup typical max ~3 s). If `BetaConfig.isProxyDaemonEnabled(context)` returns false AND `autoStartEnabled` is true, `pending` stays at zero and finish is invoked immediately — covers the edge case where the receiver has no asynchronous work to do. **(6) Permissions / manifest.** No change. `RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED` was already granted (build-178 SigDump confirms `granted=true`); the `<receiver>` declaration with the `BOOT_COMPLETED` action filter is unchanged from build 178. **(7) Behavioural contract preserved.** When the beta proxy toggle is OFF, the new `if (BetaConfig.isProxyDaemonEnabled(context))` branch is skipped entirely — no extra thread, no daemon bootstrap, identical boot behaviour to v1.0.1. The existing `auto-start projection` path is unchanged in terms of order: `startForegroundService` runs synchronously on the receiver thread as before; only the cleanup branch lost its dedicated `goAsync()` (it's now sharing the receiver's single PendingResult). **(8) What's left.** Phase 5 closes the cold-start hole exposed by Phase 4c/4d logs. Toggling the proxy ON at runtime (via Settings) still requires the user to pay the cold cost on the first call — by design, since the toggle change is rare and the UI already labels it "restart required after change".
