| **1.1.9-build175** | 175 | **Beta Engine Phase 4b — typed `pidof` replaces the `sh -c "pidof <pkg>"` fork.** Second production cash-in of the build-173 probe matrix, building directly on the build-174 Phase 4a infrastructure. Validated against the build-174 device log (`byd_log_20260521_231046.log`): Phase 4a measured 8 typed `setOverscan` calls at 11–61 ms (warm) with **zero** shell-fallback log lines, but the same log still showed 7 `beta runShell ok (Xms): pidof com.waze` lines at **48–181 ms** every ~5 s — the next obvious hot path. **(1) `Phase4Verbs.getPidsByPackage(String)`** — new pure-Java verb that opens `/proc`, iterates the numeric PID directories, reads up to 256 bytes of each `/proc/<pid>/cmdline`, splits on the first NUL to extract `argv[0]`, and collects every PID whose argv[0] equals `packageName` or starts with `"packageName:"` (Android sub-processes declared via `android:process=":foo"` in the manifest, exactly what shell `pidof` matches). Returns a space-separated list of PIDs (same wire shape as `pidof` stdout), empty string when nothing matches. Skips non-numeric `/proc` entries cheaply with a manual digit loop instead of `Integer.parseInt`/try-catch, and pre-checks `canRead()` so unreadable kernel threads don't trip `IOException`. Probe P8 (build 173) measured the same scan at **0 ms** on the BYD Seal EU with 241 live processes — replacing a 48–181 ms fork with a ~0–5 ms in-process scan. The choice of `/proc` scan over `IActivityManager.getRunningAppProcesses()` is deliberate: `/proc` is immune to the Android-10 `getRunningAppProcesses` visibility restrictions (which can return an empty list to non-system callers on some OEM builds), it doesn't need any system Context handle, and it precisely reproduces shell `pidof`'s argv[0] semantics. **(2) `ProxyDaemonMain.TXN_GET_PIDS = FIRST_CALL_TRANSACTION + 5` (=6).** Wire format: `String packageName` in → `String spaceSeparatedPids` out (or `writeException` on the unlikely scenario that the scan throws). Defensive try/catch around the scan call: any `Throwable` is unwrapped to a real `Exception` and surfaced via `writeException` so the client falls back to shell with full diagnostic context. Protocol version stays at `"2"` — purely additive transaction. **(3) `BetaProxyClient.getPidsByPackage(String) throws BetaProxyException`** — mirrors the established `runShell` / `setOverscan` Parcel pattern (`writeInterfaceToken` + `writeString` + `transact(TXN_GET_PIDS)` + `readException` + `readString`). Daemon-side exceptions surface as `BetaProxyException` so `ShellGateway` can transparently fall back to the shell path. `RemoteException` nulls the cached binder so the next call triggers a fresh connect. **(4) `ShellGateway` — typed-verb dispatch refactor.** The previous `boolean tryTypedVerb(...)` is now `String tryTypedVerb(...)` returning the stdout payload to hand to the caller's callback on success, or `null` when the command didn't match or the typed call failed. This was needed because `pidof` produces a real payload (the PID list) that the callers consume via `onSuccess(output).trim().isEmpty()` — overscan callers were unaffected because they always received `""` and continue to do so. New pattern `PIDOF = ^\\s*pidof\\s+([A-Za-z0-9._:\\-]+)\\s*$` anchored on both ends and restricted to a single argument so multi-word `pidof a b c` (and any compound invocations) fall through to shell. On match: `BetaProxyClient.getPidsByPackage(pkg)` → log `beta pidof typed ok (Xms): <pkg> → "<pids>"` → callback receives the same string the shell would have produced. **(5) Sites that benefit automatically.** Both `pidof` call sites in `MainActivity` (`reconcileDisplayState` for the cluster app, `reconcileMainDisplayState` for the main-display marker) are unchanged — they already route through `ShellGateway`. They fire every ~5 s while a cluster app or main-display app is alive (driven by `mDisplayPollHandler`). On a build-174 30-minute session, that's ~720 forks/min × 30 min × 2 sites = up to ~720 forks eliminated per session, each ~50–180 ms = **CPU and battery cycles measurable in the dashcam recording timeline**. **(6) Fallback chain still rock-solid.** typed → shell-via-daemon → legacy ADB. If `/proc` scan ever returns an unexpected empty result on a future OEM build that puts apps in cgroups invisible to uid 2000, the typed call succeeds with `""`, callers interpret that as "process dead" (legitimate semantics), and worst case the cluster bookkeeping is cleared one cycle early. If the daemon transaction itself fails (e.g. daemon dead), `BetaProxyException` triggers the existing fall-through to `runShell` → legacy. **(7) Behavioural contract preserved.** No public API removed, no source change in `MainActivity` / `ClusterService` since build 174. With the beta proxy toggle OFF the binary remains byte-for-byte identical to v1.0.1 (gateway short-circuits to `AdbLocalClient` before any pattern matching). **(8) Build.** `versionCode` 174 → 175, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"` per channel policy. No new strings, layouts, drawables, dependencies. **(9) What's left for Phase 4.** Phase 4c (probe P6 confirmed `IAutoContainer` descriptor) — direct typed transact for `AdbLocalClient.sendInfo` to bypass the BYD shell wrapper. Phase 4d (optional, probes P3/P4 to be re-confirmed against shell uid) — typed `forceStopPackage` / `killBackgroundProcesses`. |
| **1.1.9-build174** | 174 | **Beta Engine Phase 4a — typed `IWindowManager.setOverscan` replaces the `wm overscan` shell fork.** First production cash-in of the build-173 probe results (P1–P12 all PASS on BYD Seal EU). Every `wm overscan L,T,R,B -d N` and `wm overscan reset -d N` command produced by `ShellGateway` is now intercepted client-side, parsed, and routed through a typed Binder call to `IWindowManager.setOverscan(displayId, l, t, r, b)` inside the daemon — bypassing the `ProcessBuilder("sh", "-c", "wm overscan …")` JVM fork that took 70–275 ms per call in build 172 (`ClusterService:525/595`, `MainActivity.autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded`, resize SeekBar). **(1) New class `com.byd.dashcast.beta.proxy.Phase4Verbs`** (~70 LoC). Lives in the daemon process. Holds a lazy double-checked cache of the `IWindowManager` proxy and the `setOverscan(int,int,int,int,int)` `Method` so subsequent calls skip `Class.forName` / `ServiceManager.getService` / `asInterface` entirely — critical for the resize SeekBar which fires 30+ overscan changes per second while the user drags. `setOverscan(displayId, l, t, r, b) throws Throwable` is the single public verb in this build. **(2) `ProxyDaemonMain` — new `TXN_SET_OVERSCAN` (=5) transaction.** Wire format: `int displayId, int l, int t, int r, int b` in → empty reply (or `writeException` on failure). The onTransact case unwraps `InvocationTargetException` so the client receives the *real* underlying exception (typically a `SecurityException` or `IllegalArgumentException` from the framework) rather than a generic reflection wrapper. Protocol version stays at `"2"` — purely additive. **(3) `BetaProxyClient.setOverscan(int displayId, int l, int t, int r, int b) throws BetaProxyException`** — new public method mirroring the established `runShell(String)` Parcel pattern (`writeInterfaceToken` / 5× `writeInt` / `transact(TXN_SET_OVERSCAN)` / `readException`). Daemon-side exceptions surface as `BetaProxyException` so `ShellGateway` can transparently fall back. **(4) `ShellGateway` — pattern-matching typed-verb interception.** Two pre-compiled regex patterns (`WM_OVERSCAN = ^wm overscan (-?\\d+),(-?\\d+),(-?\\d+),(-?\\d+) -d (\\d+)$` and `WM_OVERSCAN_RESET = ^wm overscan reset -d (\\d+)$`) test the command before any shell call. On match: `BetaProxyClient.setOverscan(...)` is invoked; on success the original callback receives `onSuccess("")` (overscan commands produce no useful stdout) and we log `beta setOverscan typed ok (Xms): d=N L,T,R,B`. On regex miss OR typed-call exception, the code falls through to the existing `runShell` path (which itself falls back to legacy `AdbLocalClient` on its own failure). The fallback chain is now **typed → shell-via-daemon → legacy ADB** instead of the previous **shell-via-daemon → legacy ADB**, so a freshly-installed-but-OEM-modified device that rejects the typed call still ends up doing exactly what build 172 did. Anchored regex (`^…$`) on both ends so any compound or unusual command (e.g. `wm overscan … && wm size …`) cleanly falls through. **(5) Sites that benefit automatically.** All 7 production call sites migrated in build 172 already route through `ShellGateway`, so the speedup is transparent: `MainActivity` resize SeekBar (~30 calls/s while dragging), `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` (500 ms post-launch per-app overscan), `ClusterService.startActivityViaIAM` (launch-time overscan), `ClusterService.stopProjectionNoAdb` (`wm overscan reset -d 1`), `ClusterService` post-projection apply (`INSET_H,INSET_V,INSET_H,INSET_V`). The two `pidof` sites in `reconcileDisplayState` / `reconcileMainDisplayState` are **not** affected by Phase 4a (no `wm overscan` match) — they keep going through `runShell` and will be migrated in Phase 4b via `IActivityManager.getRunningAppProcesses`/`getTasks` (probe P5 PASS in build 173). **(6) Behavioural contract preserved.** No public API removed. `MainActivity` / `ClusterService` source unchanged from build 173. With the beta proxy toggle OFF the binary remains byte-for-byte identical to v1.0.1. Order of overscan calls is still preserved (the single-threaded `shell-gateway` executor stays as the serialisation point). **(7) Expected device behaviour.** The build-172 log showed `ClusterService L595` at 1687 ms (cold), `L525` at 275 ms, `MainActivity autoApplyInsets` at 84 ms, resize SeekBar at 197 ms. With Phase 4a we expect those to drop to single-digit ms after the first call warms `Phase4Verbs`' reflection cache (probe P1 measured 117 ms on the very first call due to the same cache warm-up, then 0 ms on subsequent runs). The SeekBar drag in particular should feel instantaneous. **(8) Build.** `versionCode` 173 → 174, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"` per channel policy. No new strings, layouts, drawables, dependencies. |
| **1.1.9-build173** | 173 | **Beta Engine — Phase 4 feasibility probe suite (P1–P12).** Diagnostic-only build whose sole purpose is to answer one question before we commit to the Phase 4 rewrite: *"which native AOSP APIs can the proxy daemon actually call from uid 2000 / SELinux domain `shell` on the BYD Seal EU?"* No production code path changes — `ShellGateway`, `MainActivity`, `ClusterService` behaviour is identical to build 172. **(1) New class `com.byd.dashcast.beta.proxy.Phase4Probes`** (~330 LoC). Runs **inside the daemon process** and tries every typed Binder verb that Phase 4 would replace a `sh -c "wm …"` / `am …` fork with. Twelve probes total: **P1** `IWindowManager.setOverscan(0,0,0,0,0)` — the headline verb behind every `wm overscan` shell call in the resize SeekBar and `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded`; **P2** `IWindowManager.getInitialDisplaySize(0, Point)` — read-only counterpart, useful to bypass `wm size`/`dumpsys display`; **P3** `IActivityManager.forceStopPackage("com.example.does.not.exist", -1)` — replaces `am force-stop`; **P4** `IActivityManager.killBackgroundProcesses("com.example.does.not.exist", -1)` — replaces `am kill`; **P5** `IActivityManager.getTasks(20)` (with `getRunningTasks(int)` fallback) — replaces the `dumpsys activity recents` + regex pipeline used by `ClusterService.findRunningTaskId`; **P6** raw `INTERFACE_TRANSACTION` against the `AutoContainer` system service — the missing piece to migrate `AdbLocalClient.sendInfo` away from the BYD shell wrapper; **P7** `SystemProperties.get(ro.build.version.release)` + `SystemProperties.set(debug.dashcast.probe, "1")` — read should PASS, write almost certainly fails (sepolicy), detail string carries the exact exception class; **P8** pure-Java scan of `/proc/*/cmdline` for `com.byd.dashcast` — sandbox baseline, should always PASS; if it doesn't, the daemon is sandboxed harder than expected and the entire Phase 4 plan is doomed regardless of any Binder permission; **P9** `ServiceManager.listServices()` enumeration with explicit visibility check for `window` / `activity` / `package` / `input` / `display` / `AutoContainer` / `media_session` / `audio`; **P10** `IPackageManager.getInstallerPackageName("com.byd.dashcast")` — typed PM read; **P11** `IInputManager.Stub.asInterface` + walk of `IInputManager.getDeclaredMethods()` to confirm `injectInputEvent` is reachable (already known to work via the input forwarder — sanity check); **P12** `DisplayManager.getDisplays()` via the daemon's system Context — confirms the framework managers attached to the system Context (already proven by `BetaSystemContext`) are usable from inside the daemon process too. Each probe is wrapped in a single try/catch that **unwraps `InvocationTargetException`** and categorises the failure into one of four buckets: `PASS`, `FAIL_SECURITY` (SecurityException → Phase 4 verb NOT viable from uid 2000), `FAIL_API` (`NoSuchMethodException` / `ClassNotFoundException` → API renamed on this OEM, need device-specific reflection), `FAIL_OTHER` (any other Throwable, detail carries `ClassName message`). Results are concatenated as `P1=PASS:detail|P2=FAIL_SECURITY:detail|…` with `|` and newlines stripped from detail strings to keep the parser trivial. The harness itself never crashes the daemon — any exception escaping `runAll` is caught in the binder layer and returned as `P0=FAIL_OTHER:harness …`. **(2) `ProxyDaemonMain` — new `TXN_PROBE_PHASE4` (=4) transaction.** Wire format: no args in → `String` out. The system Context (already acquired by `acquireSystemContext()` for the connect broadcast) is now also stored in `private static volatile Context sSystemContext` so `ProxyBinder.onTransact` can pass it to `Phase4Probes.runAll(...)` without redoing the reflection. Protocol version stays at `"2"` because the new transaction is purely additive — older clients keep working unchanged. **(3) `BetaProxyClient.runPhase4Probes()` — new public client method**, mirrors the `runShell(String)` pattern (`writeInterfaceToken` / `transact(TXN_PROBE_PHASE4)` / `readException` / `readString`) and rethrows as `BetaProxyException` on transact failure. **(4) `BetaTestRunner` — Family `P` and 12 new tests P1–P12.** `Family` enum extended with `P` (next to `A`, `B`, `X`). Catalog grows from 15 to 27 entries with the 12 probe rows appended after `X3`. `runOne()` switch grew a single shared case-block that dispatches every P-id to a new `testProbe(ctx, r)` helper. To avoid running the probe suite 12 times (once per row in the diag UI), the first P-test populates two static fields: `sProbeCache` (parsed `Map<String,String>` of probe-id → `"STATUS:detail"`) and `sProbeError` (set if the daemon connect or the transact itself failed). Subsequent P-tests hit the cache. The status mapping is strict: only `STATUS == "PASS"` reports green; everything else (including `FAIL_SECURITY`, `FAIL_API`, `FAIL_NULL`, `FAIL_OTHER`) is rendered as a red FAIL with the category prepended to the message so the row title in `DiagActivity` reads e.g. `FAIL_SECURITY: SecurityException Neither user 2000 nor current process has android.permission.WRITE_SECURE_SETTINGS`. The cache is reset at the start of each `runAll()` invocation so re-running the suite re-queries the daemon. **(5) Behavioural impact.** Production users see nothing — neither `ShellGateway` nor the migrated `MainActivity` / `ClusterService` call sites changed at all, and the new transaction is only triggered if the user opens **Diag → Beta Engine** and runs the suite. With the beta toggle OFF the entire P family is `FAIL` with message `daemon probe run failed: daemon not connected`, which is the expected legacy-only behaviour. **(6) What we learn from a single device run.** Each `PASS` row maps 1:1 to a Phase 4 verb that is safe to ship; each `FAIL_SECURITY` row tells us exactly which verb has to stay on the shell path (or be wrapped behind a different mechanism, e.g. a system signature shim); the `FAIL_API` taxonomy isolates OEM divergences from outright denials. The expected steady-state outcome is roughly: P8 always PASS, P2/P5/P9/P10/P11/P12 PASS, P1/P3/P4 PASS (most likely on shell uid for AOSP — to be confirmed), P6 PASS for INTERFACE_TRANSACTION only (the actual sendInfo verb still has to be triaged separately), P7 read=PASS write=FAIL. **(7) Build.** `versionCode` 172 → 173, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"` per channel policy. No new strings, layouts or drawables. No public API removed — `Phase4Probes.runAll(Context)` and `BetaProxyClient.runPhase4Probes()` are purely additive. |
| **1.1.9-build172** | 172 | **Beta Engine Phase 3 — production call-site migration to the proxy daemon.** Build 171 made the proxy daemon production-quality (12 ✓ / 0 ✗ / 3 ⊘ on device, X1 measured at 0 ms beta vs 68 ms legacy — a 60× shell-call latency win). This build cashes that win in: the seven hot-path callers of `AdbLocalClient.executeShell` / `executeShellWithResult` in `MainActivity` and `ClusterService` now route through the proxy when `beta_proxy_enabled` is ON, while staying byte-for-byte identical to v1.0.1 when the toggle is OFF. **(1) New class `com.byd.dashcast.beta.ShellGateway`** (~80 LoC). Drop-in wrapper with the same signatures as the legacy methods (`execShell(ctx, cmd)` and `execShellWithResult(ctx, cmd, AdbLocalClient.Callback)`). Owns its own dedicated single-threaded executor (`shell-gateway`, daemon thread) so call order is preserved within a process. Fast path: if `BetaConfig.isProxyDaemonEnabled(ctx)` returns `false`, delegates synchronously to `AdbLocalClient` with zero overhead. Beta path: lazily connects to the daemon via `BetaProxyClient.connect(ctx)` if needed, then calls `BetaProxyClient.runShell(cmd)` and logs `beta runShell ok (Xms): <cmd>`. On any `Throwable` (RemoteException, daemon dead, timeout, etc.) it logs a warning and transparently falls back to `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult(ctx, cmd, cb)` — the original callback is honoured by the fallback path so consumers never see the swap. **(2) `MainActivity` (4 sites migrated).** `AdbLocalClient.executeShell` → `ShellGateway.execShell` in the resize-SeekBar listener (`wm overscan w,h,w,h -d 1`) and in `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` (per-app saved overscan, fires 500 ms after launch). `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` → `ShellGateway.execShellWithResult` in `reconcileDisplayState` (`pidof <clusterPkg>` polling, runs every ~5 s while a cluster app is alive) and `reconcileMainDisplayState` (`pidof <mainPkg>` for the main-display marker). These four are the ones that fire most often in normal usage. **(3) `ClusterService` (3 sites migrated).** All three `wm overscan` system calls — the launch-time per-app overscan in `startActivityViaIAM` setup, the `stopProjectionNoAdb` reset (`wm overscan reset -d 1`), and the post-projection-active overscan apply with `INSET_H,INSET_V,INSET_H,INSET_V` on the cluster display. **(4) Sites intentionally NOT migrated.** `SysInfoActivity`, `SettingsActivity` (`Test ADB` button), `DiLink5TestRunner` and `BetaTestRunner` keep calling `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` directly because they are diagnostic / self-test surfaces that must exercise the legacy path on purpose (otherwise the test runners would compare beta against beta). `BetaProxyClient` itself keeps using `AdbLocalClient` for its own bootstrap shell (`setsid sh -c …`) and for `readDaemonLogTail` — the proxy obviously cannot route itself. **(5) Build.** `versionCode` 171 → 172, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"` per the channel policy (`1.2.0` reserved for DiLink 5). **Expected device behaviour with the toggle ON**: `wm overscan` and `pidof` calls drop from ~70 ms each to ~0 ms (single-digit ms) as measured by X1 in build 171 — most visible on the resize SeekBar (no more visible lag when dragging), on the per-app overscan auto-apply 500 ms after launch (felt as instantaneous), and on the pidof-based reconciliation timer (lower CPU per poll). **With the toggle OFF** the binary is functionally identical to v1.1.9 build 171 — every migrated site short-circuits to the same `AdbLocalClient` method it was calling before, no executor overhead, no proxy connection attempt. **Regression / fallback safety.** Any failure on the beta path (daemon down, transact failure, exception in `runShell`, etc.) is caught and logged as `ShellGateway: beta runShell failed after Xms, fallback legacy: <msg> [cmd=<cmd>]`, then the same callback is invoked through the legacy path with the same arguments. The user never sees a broken cluster operation because of a proxy hiccup. The dedicated `shell-gateway` executor uses a single thread so beta-then-legacy fallback for the same call cannot interleave with other in-flight commands. **Public API surface preserved.** `BetaProxyClient` and `AdbLocalClient` signatures unchanged. `ShellGateway` is purely additive. No layout / strings / drawable changes in this build. |
| **1.1.0-beta1** | 156 | **Beta Engine scaffolding + Diagnostics rebuild + file-based log sharing (full i18n).** First pre-release on the new `beta/1.1.0` channel. Introduces an opt-in "Beta Engine" pipeline that is wired everywhere needed but **stays inert at runtime by default** so the v1.0.1 stable code path remains the source of truth. **(1) New `com.byd.dashcast.beta` package** (5 classes, ~654 LoC). `BetaConfig` (67 LoC) — SharedPreferences-backed (`byd_app_prefs`, same store as `SettingsActivity`) feature flags: `beta_engine_enabled` and `beta_proxy_enabled` (both default `false`). Granularity = option B (two independent toggles). Activation semantics = next launch (toggles are read once at process start by `BetaSystemContext.refresh()`, and a restart-required dialog is shown when the user flips them). `BetaSystemContext` (119 LoC) — process-wide singleton snapshot of the beta state, exposes `isEngineEnabled()` / `isProxyEnabled()` to the rest of the app without re-reading prefs in hot paths. `BetaProxyClient` (94 LoC) — stub client that will eventually route ADB calls through an alternate transport; currently returns `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` for every probe. `BetaEngineGateway` (65 LoC) — single entry point used by future call-sites, exposes a `safeCall(legacy, beta)` helper that **auto-falls back to the legacy implementation on any exception** so the user can keep using the app even if the beta path fails. `BetaTestRunner` (309 LoC) — 15-test self-check matrix (B1–B6 real beta-engine probes, A1–A6 currently `SKIPPED` placeholders for the ADB-bridge work, X1–X3 mixed cross-checks), each test returns a `Result` with status (`PASS` / `FAIL` / `SKIPPED` / `WARN`), latency in ms and a short message. **(2) `SettingsActivity` (+36 LoC).** New "Beta (expérimental)" card containing two `MaterialSwitch` rows (`sw_beta_engine`, `sw_beta_proxy`), an amber warning banner (`bg_beta_warning` drawable, `bg_beta_pill` for the "BETA" pill) explicitly stating the beta nature, and a restart-required `AlertDialog` triggered whenever either switch is toggled. The card is **always visible** (no hidden gesture, no developer mode required) because the v1.1.0 line is a beta channel and the user opted-in by installing the beta APK. Toggle changes persist through `BetaConfig.set*()` but have **no runtime ADB effect yet** — flipping them off restores the exact v1.0.1 behaviour, flipping them on currently only enables the diagnostics readouts and the auto-fallback path in `BetaEngineGateway`. **(3) `DiagActivity` rebuilt as a 6-tab `TabLayout`** (~257 LoC). Tabs in order: **Beta Engine** (default selected, runs the 15-test `BetaTestRunner` and renders a colour-coded card list via `item_beta_test.xml` + `include_diag_beta_engine.xml`, with a "Lancer tous" / "Run all" button and a "Envoyer (.log)" / "Send (.log)" button), Système, Cluster, ADB, Logs, Apps. The five legacy tabs share a single `include_diag_coming_soon.xml` placeholder that prints "Bientôt disponible" — the existing 1.0.1 diag screens are kept reachable via the overflow menu and will be migrated tab by tab in subsequent beta drops. **(4) `AppLogger` refactor (file-based sharing everywhere, +file API).** Added `writeFile(ctx, prefix, content)` — generic timestamped `.log` writer under `getExternalFilesDir("logs")` with a `files-path` fallback; added `shareFile(ctx, File, subject, chooserTitle)` — generic `Intent.ACTION_SEND` with `EXTRA_STREAM` via the existing FileProvider authority (`${packageName}.fileprovider`, `xml/file_paths.xml` covers both `external-files-path` and `files-path`) and `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION`. The existing `share(ctx)` is now file-first (attaches a `byd_log_<timestamp>.log`) with a plain-text fallback only when the file write fails. New `shareWithReport(ctx, reportText)` writes a combined `byd_report_<ts>.log` (header line + diagnostic report + full log buffer) and attaches it via `shareFile`. Net effect: **every "Send logs" / "Send report" action in the app now ships a real `.log` file** instead of a raw text body, which fixes the previous 64 KB-ish silent truncation seen on Android share-sheets and lets the user attach the file to GitHub issues / e-mail / WhatsApp without copy-paste loss. **(5) New layouts and drawables.** `layout/include_diag_beta_engine.xml`, `layout/include_diag_coming_soon.xml`, `layout/item_beta_test.xml`, `drawable/bg_beta_pill.xml`, `drawable/bg_beta_warning.xml`, and edits to `layout/activity_diag.xml` (TabLayout root) and `layout/activity_settings.xml` (Beta card insertion). **(6) Strings — full i18n.** Added a block of 28 new keys ending with `diag_beta_run_all` / `diag_beta_copy_report` / `diag_beta_report_copied` to `values/strings.xml` (FR default) and `values-en/strings.xml`. Translated all 28 keys to the 10 remaining locales (`ar`, `be`, `de`, `es`, `it`, `kk`, `ru`, `tr`, `uk`, `uz`) via `scripts/inject_beta_strings.py` (idempotent — re-runs are no-ops thanks to a `diag_beta_run_all` marker check). 12 locales total, parity with the existing UI. **(7) Build.** `versionCode` 155 → 156, `versionName` `"1.0.1"` → `"1.1.0-beta1"`. No business logic from v1.0.1 is modified — cluster projection, overscan, OTA, restore-origin-cluster, app shortcuts, category filters, reconnect popup and visual mode all behave exactly as in v1.0.1 with both beta switches OFF. **Known limitations of this drop**: A1–A6 tests show `SKIPPED`; the proxy ADB transport is a stub; the legacy 5 diag tabs render a placeholder. These are intentional and will be filled in subsequent `1.1.0-betaN` drops on the same channel. |
| **0.9.61-alpha** | 127 | **Settings icons — drop the style attribute entirely.** Even with `android:layout_width="28dp"`/`layout_height="28dp"` inlined on the View tag in 0.9.6, the leading icons still rendered as tiny white dots on the BYD device. Compared to the `+`/`−` slider icons (which render correctly), the only remaining difference was that the leading `<ImageView>` still had `style="@style/Widget.DashCast.ListItem.Leading"` applied. Removed the `style="…"` attribute from the 7 leading `<ImageView>` instances in `activity_settings.xml` and inlined every needed attribute directly on the View tag (`layout_width=28dp`, `layout_height=28dp`, `layout_marginEnd=16dp`, `layout_gravity=center_vertical`, `scaleType=fitCenter`, `tint=@color/md_on_surface_variant`, plus the existing `src`). No other change. |
| **0.9.6-alpha** | 126 | **Settings icons — the actual root cause.** Reported on the car after 0.9.5: the icons in `Comportement` and `À propos` were rendering as tiny white dots, regardless of the `android:tint` value. Root cause finally found: on this BYD Android 10 runtime, `android:layout_width` and `android:layout_height` declared inside a `<style>` (here `Widget.DashCast.ListItem.Leading` with `28dp × 28dp`) are not honored by `LayoutInflater` because layout params are extracted from the View tag itself, not from the applied style. The ImageView therefore fell back to a near-zero size, so the vector was rendered as a 1–2 px white dot. Fix: inlined `android:layout_width="28dp"`, `android:layout_height="28dp"`, `android:layout_marginEnd="16dp"` on every leading `<ImageView>` in `activity_settings.xml` (7 occurrences). The style still applies `layout_gravity`, `scaleType` and other non-layout attributes. No other change. |
| **0.9.5-alpha** | 125 | **Settings icons — third and final fix attempt.** In 0.9.3 the tint was on the vector drawable (didn't work). In 0.9.4 it was inlined as `android:tint="?attr/colorOnSurfaceVariant"` on each `<ImageView>` (still didn't work on the BYD device — the theme attribute reference returned a transparent value at runtime, so every leading icon in Comportement, À propos and the +/- slider icons rendered as empty space). Final fix: replaced every `android:tint="?attr/colorOnSurfaceVariant"` with `android:tint="@color/md_on_surface_variant"` (8 occurrences in `activity_settings.xml`) and `android:tint="?attr/colorOnPrimaryContainer"` with `android:tint="@color/md_on_primary_container"` on the Welcome hero `ic_cast`. The color resource is defined in both `values/colors.xml` (`#43474E`) and `values-night/colors.xml` (`#C3C6CF`), so DayNight is still respected through resource-qualifier resolution instead of theme-attribute indirection — which is what actually works on this Android 10 BYD runtime. No other change. |
| **0.9.4-alpha** | 124 | **Mockup fidelity & OTA parity fixes on top of 0.9.3.** **Settings — leading icons now visible (real fix)**: in 0.9.3 the `?attr/colorOnSurfaceVariant` tint was moved from `<vector>` drawables to the `Widget.DashCast.ListItem.Leading` style, but on the BYD device the runtime never propagated that theme-attribute tint through the style either, so the row icons in `Comportement` and `À propos` were still invisible. Root-cause fix: removed `android:tint="?attr/colorOnSurfaceVariant"` from the 8 affected vector drawables (`ic_add`, `ic_autorenew`, `ic_filter_list`, `ic_info_outline`, `ic_remove`, `ic_restart_alt`, `ic_save`, `ic_system_update`) and from `ic_cast.xml` (`?attr/colorOnPrimaryContainer`), removed the tint item from the `Leading` style, and inlined `android:tint="?attr/colorOnSurfaceVariant"` directly on every leading `<ImageView>` in `activity_settings.xml` (7 occurrences across the cluster type, overscan, behavior and about cards, plus the science pre-release row). The hero `ic_cast` in `activity_welcome.xml` got an inline `android:tint="?attr/colorOnPrimaryContainer"`. **Settings — `Vérifier` button now triggers the real OTA flow**: in 0.9.3 the `btn_check_update` opened the GitHub Releases page in a browser, which did not match the 3-dot-menu OTA. Refactored `MainActivity.makeOtaProgressListener(boolean)` (instance method, ~125 lines) into `public static makeOtaProgressListener(Activity, boolean)` by replacing every `MainActivity.this` / `getString` / `getColor` / `getResources()` / `isFinishing()` / `isDestroyed()` with the passed `activity` reference. Both existing internal callsites (`MainActivity.onCreate` auto-check at launch and `showOverflowMenu` case 6) were updated to pass the activity. `SettingsActivity.btn_check_update` now calls `UpdateChecker.checkUpdate(this, MainActivity.makeOtaProgressListener(this, true))` — same `AlertDialog` with `ProgressBar` for download, same `startDownload` / `onInstalling` / `onUpToDate` / `onError` handlers as the 3-dot menu. `row_source_code` still opens `https://github.com/Kiroha/byd-dashcast` via `openUrl()`. The supporting text below `Rechercher une mise à jour` was rewritten from `Ouvre la page des releases sur GitHub` to `Vérifie si une nouvelle version est disponible`. **Welcome card — full-width + Continue button**: the card was capped at 800dp in 0.9.3 and still felt narrow; it now uses `match_parent` with 60dp horizontal padding so it spans the full screen, the hero square shrinks from 96dp to 80dp, the title from 44sp to 36sp, the language-select prompt from 16sp to 15sp, vertical paddings/margins are tightened so the four rows of three language buttons fit without scrolling on the 12.3" cluster. Added a new `Widget.DashCast.Button.Text` button `btn_continue_without_change` with the `ic_arrow_forward` trailing icon: tapping it calls `LocaleHelper.markSetupDone(this)` and starts `MainActivity` without changing the locale (matches the mockup's `welcome_continue_without_change` action). **Settings — full i18n pass**: extracted 13 remaining hardcoded French strings into `values/strings.xml` (`settings_section_behavior`, `settings_section_about`, `settings_cluster_88_desc`, `settings_cluster_123_desc`, `settings_cluster_1025_desc`, `settings_boot_auto_start_support`, `settings_reconnect_popup_support`, `settings_category_filters_support`, `settings_about_version_label`, `settings_check_update_title`, `settings_check_update_support`, `settings_check_update_button`, `settings_source_code_title`) and translated them into the 12 supported locales (fr, en, de, es, it, tr, ru, uk, ar, uz, kk, be). The `welcome_continue_without_change` key was also added for the new button in the same 12 locales. No business logic changed: cluster type, overscan, behavior toggles, pre-release channel, visual mode and OTA install still save and load exactly as before. |
| **0.9.3-alpha** | 123 | **Mockup fidelity fixes on Welcome & Settings (follow-up to 0.9.2)**. **Welcome**: the language-picker card was shrinking to the intrinsic width of the button grid because the outer `MaterialCardView` was `wrap_content`; it now uses an explicit `800dp` width (matching the mockup's `max-width: 800px`), and the inner `LinearLayout` is `match_parent`, so the 12 outlined language buttons (`weight=1`) actually fill the card from edge to edge as in `mockups/mockup_m3.html` screen #1. **Settings — leading icons now visible**: the per-row icons in the `Comportement` and `À propos` cards were defined in XML but invisible at runtime because the `?attr/colorOnSurfaceVariant` tint was declared inside each `<vector>` drawable, where theme attribute resolution is unreliable across Android versions. The tint moved to the `Widget.DashCast.ListItem.Leading` style (applied on the `ImageView`), where AppCompat resolves theme attributes reliably; `scaleType=fitCenter` was added so the 24dp glyphs render centered inside the 28dp slot. **Settings — À propos card rebuilt to match the mockup**: now contains four rows: (1) `Version` (`info` icon, dynamic `BuildConfig` value); (2) **`Rechercher une mise à jour`** (`system_update` icon + text-button `Vérifier` `btn_check_update`, opens `https://github.com/Kiroha/byd-dashcast/releases`); (3) **`Code source`** (`code` icon + trailing `open_in_new` chevron, row id `row_source_code`, full-row ripple via `?attr/selectableItemBackground`, opens `https://github.com/Kiroha/byd-dashcast`); (4) the pre-release `MaterialSwitch` `cb_prerelease` (icon switched from `system_update` to `science` to avoid duplicating the update-check glyph). Added three vector drawables: `ic_code`, `ic_open_in_new`, `ic_science`. Added a null- and intent-safe `openUrl()` helper in `SettingsActivity` (`Intent.ACTION_VIEW`, `FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK`, Toast fallback). All existing widget IDs (`rg_cluster_type`, `rb_88/123/1025`, `sb_inset_h/v`, `tv_inset_h/v_value`, `cb_visual_mode`, `cb_boot_auto_start`, `cb_show_category_filters`, `cb_reconnect_popup`, `cb_prerelease`, `ll_sliders_mode`, `btn_apply_overscan`, `btn_reset_overscan`, `tv_overscan_result`, `btn_apply_top`, `tv_version_value`) preserved. No business logic was modified — only layout, styles, drawables, and three new click bindings. |
| **0.9.2-alpha** | 122 | **M3 Mockup-Accurate Redesign — Welcome + Settings**. **Welcome screen**: rebuilt from the mockup verbatim with a 96dp tonal hero square containing a new `cast_connected` vector glyph (`drawable/ic_cast.xml`), a large 44sp "DashCast" title, a primary-tinted dynamic version subtitle (`BYD CLUSTER MIRROR · v<versionName>`, populated from `BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME` in `WelcomeActivity.onCreate`), an `md_surface_container` card (28dp corners, 56/80dp padding, 0 elevation), and the 12 language buttons rendered as outlined pills (`Widget.DashCast.Button.Outlined`, 14dp gap). **Settings screen**: full mockup-fidelity rebuild as a true two-column layout. New top bar with title and a tonal `Appliquer` button (`btn_apply_top`, mirrors the in-card Apply action). Left column = cluster type (3 list-items: radio + headline + supporting text inside a single 20dp `MaterialCardView` on `md_surface_container`) + overscan margins (compact visual-mode switch row at the top, then per-axis Vertical / Horizontal sliders with leading `remove` / trailing `add` icons and a `colorPrimaryContainer` value badge, plus the existing visual D-pad mode, plus the Filled / Outlined Apply / Reset button pair and the monospace result panel). Right column = behaviour toggles (`MaterialSwitch` list-items with leading icons `restart_alt`, `autorenew`, `filter_list` — `cb_boot_auto_start`, `cb_reconnect_popup`, `cb_show_category_filters`) + about card (info icon + dynamic version label `0.9.2 · build 122`, system_update icon + pre-release `MaterialSwitch` `cb_prerelease`). New uppercase section headers in `colorPrimary` with 0.15 letter-spacing matching the mockup. **All existing widget IDs preserved** (`rg_cluster_type`, `rb_88/123/1025`, `sb_inset_h/v`, `tv_inset_h/v_value`, `cb_visual_mode`, `cb_boot_auto_start`, `cb_show_category_filters`, `cb_reconnect_popup`, `cb_prerelease`, `ll_sliders_mode`, `btn_apply_overscan`, `btn_reset_overscan`, `tv_overscan_result`). New IDs are purely cosmetic (`btn_apply_top`, `tv_version_value`). **SettingsActivity.java**: changed the `cb*` field type from `CheckBox` to `CompoundButton` so the layout can swap to `MaterialSwitch` without breaking the cast (both inherit `setChecked` / `isChecked` / `setOnCheckedChangeListener`). Added the top-bar `Apply` click binding and the dynamic version text. Added 8 new vector drawables (`ic_cast`, `ic_restart_alt`, `ic_autorenew`, `ic_filter_list`, `ic_info_outline`, `ic_remove`, `ic_add`, `ic_save`, `ic_system_update`) and a `bg_value_badge` shape. Added the reusable list-item styles in `styles_m3.xml` (`Widget.DashCast.SectionHead`, `Widget.DashCast.ListItem`, `Widget.DashCast.ListItem.Leading/Content/Headline/Support`, `Widget.DashCast.ValueBadge`). No business logic was modified — cluster type, overscan, boot-auto-start, reconnect popup, category filters, pre-release channel and visual mode all save and load exactly as before. |
| **0.9.1-alpha** | 120 | **Material 3 Migration — Phases 2 & 3 (Reusable Widgets + Welcome & Settings)**: Phase 2 adds a complete library of reusable Material 3 widget styles in `values/styles_m3.xml` (`Widget.DashCast.Button.Filled` / `.Tonal` / `.Outlined` / `.Text` / `.Error` / `.Small`, `Widget.DashCast.IconButton` and `.IconButton.Filled`, `Widget.DashCast.Fab` and `.Fab.Extended`, `Widget.DashCast.Card.Filled` / `.Outlined` / `.Elevated` with 16dp corners and 20dp content padding, `Widget.DashCast.Switch` / `.CheckBox` / `.RadioButton`, `Widget.DashCast.Chip.Filter` / `.Assist`, plus M3 text appearances `TextAppearance.DashCast.HeadlineSmall` / `TitleLarge` / `TitleMedium` / `BodyLarge` / `BodyMedium` / `SectionHeader`). No existing layout is touched in phase 2, so it is a zero-regression refactor. Phase 3 migrates the **Welcome screen** (`activity_welcome.xml`) and the **Settings screen** (`activity_settings.xml`) to Material 3: hard-coded dark backgrounds (`#0D0D0D`, `#1A237E`) and `@color/bg_main` / `@color/bg_card` are replaced by M3 attributes (`?attr/colorSurface`, `?attr/colorSurfaceVariant`, `?attr/colorOnSurface`, `?attr/colorOnSurfaceVariant`, `?attr/colorPrimary`), each section is now rendered inside a `MaterialCardView` with 16dp rounded corners and tonal surface, all 12 language buttons of the welcome screen use the new tonal style (`Widget.DashCast.Button.Tonal`), the apply/reset overscan buttons become a filled / tonal pair, all checkboxes and radio buttons are upgraded to `MaterialCheckBox` / `MaterialRadioButton` with M3 typography, and the overscan result panel uses the new `md_surface_container_highest` token. **Critically, every `android:id` is preserved** (`btn_lang_fr/en/de/it/tr/es/ru/uk/ar/uz/kk/be`, `rg_cluster_type`, `rb_88/123/1025`, `sb_inset_h/v`, `tv_inset_h/v_value`, `cb_visual_mode`, `cb_boot_auto_start`, `cb_show_category_filters`, `cb_reconnect_popup`, `cb_prerelease`, `ll_sliders_mode`, `btn_apply_overscan`, `btn_reset_overscan`, `tv_overscan_result`) so `WelcomeActivity.java` and `SettingsActivity.java` keep working without any code change. No business logic was modified, only XML structure and styling. |
| **0.9.0-alpha** | 119 | **Material 3 Migration — Phase 1 (Foundation)**: First step of a phased migration to Material Design 3 matching `mockups/mockup_m3.html`. Bumped `compileSdkVersion` from 29 to 33 (`targetSdkVersion` kept at 29, runtime behavior on the Seal is unchanged). Added the `com.google.android.material:material:1.9.0` dependency. Switched `AppTheme` parent from `Theme.AppCompat.DayNight.DarkActionBar` to `Theme.Material3.DayNight.NoActionBar`. Introduced a full Material 3 color token palette (`md_primary`, `md_on_primary`, `md_primary_container`, `md_surface_container_*`, `md_outline`, etc.) in both `values/colors.xml` (light scheme) and `values-night/colors.xml` (dark scheme matching the mockup, source color `#1565C0`). All legacy color names (`bg_main`, `text_primary`, `colorPrimary`, ...) and every existing layout are left untouched, so this phase introduces no functional or layout regression — only the underlying theme system is upgraded so that subsequent phases can migrate the screens one by one to Material 3 components and visuals. |
| **0.8.7-alpha** | 118 | **UX: Reconnect Popup Default Off + i18n**: The cluster reconnect popup is now disabled by default (opt-in via Settings) to avoid intrusive prompts for new users. Added `settings_reconnect_popup` translation string in all 12 supported locales (fr, en, de, es, it, ar, be, kk, ru, tr, uk, uz). Corrected French wording. |
| **0.8.6-alpha** | 117 | **Feature: Reconnect Popup Opt-In + FloatingRemoteButton Race Fix**: Made the "relaunch last app on cluster reconnect" popup fully opt-in via a new checkbox in Settings (`PREF_RECONNECT_POPUP`). Fixed a race condition in `FloatingRemoteButton` where `show()` was silently lost when called before the overlay was ready — added `sShouldBeVisible` static flag with deferred visibility after `createOverlay()` and `onStart()` re-sync. |
| **0.7.8-alpha** | 96 | **Boot/Recovery Safety**: Persist session cluster package tracking in SharedPreferences (`session_cluster_pkgs`) so state survives full car shutdown. Add boot-time cleanup of display affinity when auto-start projection is disabled (`boot_auto_start_enabled=false`). Add `MainActivity.onCreate` safety-net cleanup path. Keep cleanup disabled when auto-start projection is enabled to avoid user-facing regressions. |
| **0.7.7-alpha** | 95 | **Features + UX + Display Affinity**: Add category filters (All / Navigation / Media) with Settings toggle. Add floating quick-switch popup (recent cluster apps). Add profile launch mode (Driving/Parking app picker). Add per-app usage statistics dialog. Replace cluster-unavailable toast with activation dialog offering immediate projection start and deferred app launch. Move tracked apps back to Display 0 on projection stop and before red-cross kill to prevent apps relaunching on hidden VirtualDisplay. |
| **0.7.6-alpha** | 94 | **Theme & Clarity**: Fix DayNight follow-system behavior by setting default night mode at application startup. Improve Settings wording clarity for visual adjustment mode. |
| **0.7.5-alpha** | 93 | **Stability & Cleanup**: Fix shortcut crash (`LauncherApps.startShortcut` replaces broken `startActivity`). Fix BootReceiver SharedPrefs mismatch (auto-boot toggle was broken). Fix Settings locale not applied. Fix premature APK delete in UpdateChecker. Fix redirect depth limit in OTA. Fix TeleNav ghost-nav black screen. Fix FloatingRemoteButton handler leak. Full dark mode color pass (70+ hardcoded colors replaced). Remove dead code (`stopProjection`, orphan resources). |
| **0.7.4-alpha** | 92 | **Feature**: Native Android App Shortcuts support (list + grid view with popup menu). Smart app categorization (Navigation / Media / Others). Translated category labels (12 locales). |
| **0.7.3-alpha** | 91 | **Feature / UX**: Smart sorting logic for launched apps. Complete transition to Dynamic DayNight theme for comfortable night driving. Live, visually-scaled Overscan configuration mode. Auto-Boot receiver allows the app to automatically start the Cluster projection service when the car powers on. Dead code & UI diagnostics cleanup. New features translated to 12 languages. |
| **0.5.1** | 78 | **Release**: Comprehensive i18n support localized hardcoded strings across UI, overscan layouts, and system Toasts in all 12 supported languages. Fixed missing "Adjust" button from the manual UI diagram. |
| **0.4.3-alpha** | 73 | **Enhancement / Fix**: Removed Native Library extraction (\`extractNativeLibs=false\`) to drastically reduce install time and zero-out duplicate storage load. Corrected the URI Share Permission flags across all FileProvider Intents so Logs and Sniffers export successfully without crashing the OS. |
| **0.4.2-alpha** | 72 | **Critical BugFix**: Re-engineered ADB Task Identifier extractor using \`awk\` pattern matching to properly correlate BYD's multiline \`dumpsys\` outputs, ensuring the Red Cross accurately finds and removes orphaned Tasks in the Recents pool. |
| **0.4.1-alpha** | 71 | **Revert**: The red-cross kill button no longer drops the cluster projection. It only stops the app memory process, leaving the cluster display ready (black screen) for a new app cast without needing to restart the whole projection layer. |
| **0.4.0-alpha** | 70 | **Critical BugFix**: Resolved the red-cross force-stop on DiLink 3.0 via strict `app_process64` syntax. When killing an app currently running, DashCast will now automatically release the cluster and return back to the standard BYD Speedometer to prevent the black frozen screen illusion. |
| **0.3.3-alpha** | 69 | **Fix**: Corrected ADB kill app action (`TaskRemover` headless invocation syntax) and made mirror dynamic \`Apply\` button utilize \`wm overscan\` like the settings. |
| **0.3.2-alpha** | 68 | **Diag: ADAS Toggle** — Added two direct ADB buttons in the Diag tool to manually and easily send `sendInfo(1000, 47)` (ON) and `sendInfo(1000, 48)` (OFF) to toggle BYD's secret ADAS debugging mode on the cluster. |
| **0.3.1-alpha** | 67 | **Optimisation: Elegant App Killer** — Replaced the blind Binder sequence (service call 23/24) with a custom headless Java process (TaskRemover). DashCast now uses Android's native reflection inside an ADB shell to gracefully and accurately find the correct removeTask function regardless of the DiLink version or OEM tweaks. |
| **0.3.0-alpha** | 66 | **Feature: Per-App Resizing!** — You can now save individual Horizontal & Vertical inset values for each application separately using quick-access sliders directly accessible under the Mirror preview panel in DashCast, while the target app is running! (No more global resizing conflicts between Media and Navigation apps) |
| **0.2.95-alpha** | 65 | **Hotfix 5: Ghost Apps in Recents (Android 10/DiLink 3.0)** — Fixed the regex matching for ADB task removal. DashCast now successfully identifies and removes the active Task ID from the App Manager (Recents) so DiLink no longer mistakenly considers the app as running in the background after clicking the Red Cross. |
| **0.2.94-alpha** | 64 | **Hotfix 4: Kill Button Behavior** — Fixed an issue where terminating the active app with the red cross also stopped the ClusterService projection engine. The VirtualDisplay is now left intact when killing an app, allowing the user to seamlessly launch another app to the dashboard without needing to re-activate the engine. Note: Original fix failed injection sync. |
| **0.2.93-alpha** | 63 | **Hotfix 3: Kill Button Behavior** — Fixed an issue where terminating the active app with the red cross also stopped the ClusterService projection engine. The VirtualDisplay is now left intact when killing an app, allowing the user to seamlessly launch another app to the dashboard without needing to re-activate the engine. |
| **0.2.92-alpha** | 62 | **Hotfix 2: Android 10 am task compatibility** — Further fallbacks added to delete the ghost tasks in Android UI. Attempts `am task rm`, `am stack remove` or a low-level Binder call `service call activity 23` depending on ROM customizations. Warnings suppressed to allow the final `am force-stop` to execute cleanly. |
| **0.2.91-alpha** | 61 | **Hotfix: Android 10 am task compatibility** — Replaced `am task rm` with the formally valid `am task remove` command to ensure the Recents UI ghostly task is successfully deleted before `am force-stop`. |
| **0.2.9-alpha** | 60 | **Process & Task Killer** — Fixed an issue where terminating an app with the red cross only killed its background processes but left it in the 'Recent Apps' overview. ADB now identifies `TaskRecord` IDs and executes `am task rm` to fully clear them from Recents UI before issuing `force-stop`. |
| **0.2.8-alpha** | 59 | **Interactive OTA UI** — Enhanced the Over-The-Air updater. Users are now presented with a dialog displaying the full changelog natively fetched from GitHub releases. Added explicit 'Update Now' vs 'Update Later' choices rather than forced auto-download. |
| **0.2.7-alpha** | 58 | **Auto-Launch Checkbox Fix** — Restored visibility and binding logic for the 'Auto' checkbox in the list/grid views. |
| **0.2.6-alpha** | 57 | **Floating Button Fix** — Fixed Smart Overlay Button spawn bug (immediate transparency and off-screen misplacement). Elevated opacity and timing logic to restore correct visibility sync. |
| **0.2.5-alpha** | 56 | **Dark Text Fix** — Grid mode app labels corrected to Dark Gray for visibility against light backgrounds. Duplicate checkbox fixed. |
| **0.2.4-alpha** | 55 | **Auto-Launch & Grid View** — Implemented 5-column Grid layout. Added Boot Auto-Launch exclusivity (Radio behavior) and "Favorites" persistence via Long-Press. |
| **0.2.3-alpha** | 54 | **QoL UI Update** — Removed Split-screen logic. Added Top Overflow menu with List/Grid toggle switch. Persistent state management via SharedPreferences. |

| **0.1.31-alpha** | 32 | **DiLink5 resize test (Section 7)** — Isolated IATM test in DiagActivity: WM:4→5 + `resizeTask(RESIZE_MODE_FORCED=3)` via raw Binder on `activity_task`. Sanity: lint fixes (WrongConstant, DefaultLocale, MissingTranslation ×4 locales, RedundantLabel). |
| **0.1.30-alpha** | 31 | **Strings + UI cleanup** — Remove cluster keyboard button. Remove ADAS diagnostic buttons (cmd 12/13 confirmed without effect on 2D cluster Seal EU). String cleanup ×5 locales. |
| **0.1.29-alpha** | 30 | **Settings: overscan tuning** — New SettingsActivity screen: H/V inset tuning with immediate apply. Rename main screen buttons for clarity (Activate / Stop / Restore Original). |
| **0.1.28-alpha** | 29 | **ADAS diagnostic buttons** — Diag section 3: ADAS ON/OFF buttons (cmd 12/13) for 3D cluster testing. |
| **0.1.27-alpha** | 28 | **Bounds via `wm overscan`** — Replace dead IATM bounds approach with `wm overscan -d <displayId>`. Safety guard: restrict overscan to cluster display only + reset button for display 0. |
| **0.1.26-alpha** | 27 | **State persistence + storage cleanup** — Persist active cluster app to survive Activity recreation (SharedPreferences). Diag section 6: storage cleanup (logs, reports, sniffer files, log buffer). FREEFORM bounds: retry via IATM after launch. |
| **0.1.25-alpha** | 26 | **Logs menu + i18n refactor** — Overflow menu: new "📜 Logs" entry → LogActivity. Remove 69 dead strings. Migrate DiagActivity to fully localized resources. |
| **0.1.24-alpha** | 25 | **Sanity #1+#2** — Dead code and dead fields removed. `ClusterMirrorManager` blank line fix. DiagActivity: remove obsolete Freedom tests, unify comment numbering [1]-[5]. |
| **0.1.23-alpha** | 24 | **`moveTaskToDisplay` + kill detection** — Move tasks between displays without force-stop + relaunch. `OnUidImportanceListener` detects external app kill and clears stale cluster state. |
| **0.1.22-alpha** | 23 | **Mirror shortcut + overlay refactor** — Replace GPS floating button with 📺 mirror shortcut. New 📺 status bar button (visible when an app is on the cluster). Remove floating log overlay button. |
| **0.1.21-alpha** | 22 | **Security audit + Telegram share** — Full display-operation audit: guard `wm overscan`, `am display`, ADB commands to cluster display only. Long-press on any result TextView → share to Telegram. |
| **0.1.20-alpha** | 21 | **RE Sniffer fix + FREEFORM bounds** — RE Sniffer: `setsid` prefix, header sync, logcat without process filter. FREEFORM inset bounds: prevent app clipping at cluster screen edges. Fix: stop unintended `AppStartManagement` in fallback activation path. |
| **0.1.19-alpha** | 20 | **AutoDisplayService control + sanity** — Binder control of AutoDisplayService. Resource leak fixes. Sanity checks #5→#8 complete. README updated. |
| **0.1.18-alpha** | 19 | **DiagActivity refactor + DashCastDaemon** — Remove Freedom diagnostic tests. Add Headless System RE Sniffer (deep dumpsys + broadcast tracking). Add DashCastDaemon (`app_process` VirtualDisplay probe) and TEST 14-16. |
| **0.1.17-alpha** | 18 | **Complete Freedom removal + sanity** — Remove `checkAndStartWithFreedom()` and all `FreedomStatus` code from ClusterService/MainActivity. BYD native cluster spawn via `AppStartManagement`. Sanity #5-#7: empty catches logged, dead code, JMM fixes. |
| **0.1.16-alpha** | 17 | **Sanity + test build 04/05/2026** — Dead code removal, duplicate `setEnabled` fix, DiagActivity comment cleanup. Internal test build bump versionCode 17. |
| **0.1.15-alpha** | 16 | **Overlay cleanup + DashCastDaemon fix** — Remove floating log overlay button. Fix DashCastDaemon: bypass packageName UID mismatch preventing `app_process` daemon start. |
| **0.1.14-alpha** | 15 | **Display 0 security** — Full audit: all display-targeted operations (`wm overscan`, ADB shell commands) restricted to cluster display. Prevent accidental main screen modification. |
| **0.1.13-alpha** | 14 | **Mirror shortcut in status bar** — 📺 button appears in the cluster control panel when an app is active on the cluster. Long-press result TextViews → share to Telegram. |
| **0.1.12-alpha** | 13 | **FREEFORM inset bounds + RE Sniffer** — Inset margins applied via `setLaunchBounds` to prevent app clipping on cluster edges. RE Sniffer: `setsid`, header sync, raw logcat mode. |

| **0.1.10-alpha** | 11 | **DiagActivity refactor** — Remove Freedom-dependent tests. Add Headless System Sniffer (logcat, `am monitor`, `dumpsys` via ADB). Sanity check #7: orphan imports, dead strings. |
| **0.1.9-alpha** | 10 | **Freedom removal** — Complete removal of Freedom (com.xdja.clusterdemo) from codebase. BYD native cluster spawn via `com.byd.appstartmanagement`. Sanity #6: JMM correctness, dead code cleanup. |
| **0.1.8-alpha** | 9 | **Sanity checks #1–#5** — Empty catches now logged (no more silent failures), dead methods removed, orphan imports cleaned. |
| **0.1.7-alpha** | 8 | **Freedom-free** — VirtualDisplay cluster creation confirmed via live logcat RE (sendInfo 30→16→35). Full autonomous activation sequence. Sanity fixes (duplicate setEnabled, color reset). README cleaned. |

## Internal versions (pre-public)

| Version | versionCode | Summary |
|---------|-------------|---------|
| **0.5.1** | 78 | **Release**: Comprehensive i18n support localized hardcoded strings across UI, overscan layouts, and system Toasts in all 12 supported languages. Fixed missing "Adjust" button from the manual UI diagram. |
| **0.4.3-alpha** | 73 | **Enhancement / Fix**: Removed Native Library extraction (\`extractNativeLibs=false\`) to drastically reduce install time and zero-out duplicate storage load. Corrected the URI Share Permission flags across all FileProvider Intents so Logs and Sniffers export successfully without crashing the OS. |
| **0.4.2-alpha** | 72 | **Critical BugFix**: Re-engineered ADB Task Identifier extractor using \`awk\` pattern matching to properly correlate BYD's multiline \`dumpsys\` outputs, ensuring the Red Cross accurately finds and removes orphaned Tasks in the Recents pool. |
| **0.4.1-alpha** | 71 | **Revert**: The red-cross kill button no longer drops the cluster projection. It only stops the app memory process, leaving the cluster display ready (black screen) for a new app cast without needing to restart the whole projection layer. |
| **0.4.0-alpha** | 70 | **Critical BugFix**: Resolved the red-cross force-stop on DiLink 3.0 via strict `app_process64` syntax. When killing an app currently running, DashCast will now automatically release the cluster and return back to the standard BYD Speedometer to prevent the black frozen screen illusion. |
| **0.3.3-alpha** | 69 | **Fix**: Corrected ADB kill app action (`TaskRemover` headless invocation syntax) and made mirror dynamic \`Apply\` button utilize \`wm overscan\` like the settings. |
| **0.3.2-alpha** | 68 | **Diag: ADAS Toggle** — Added two direct ADB buttons in the Diag tool to manually and easily send `sendInfo(1000, 47)` (ON) and `sendInfo(1000, 48)` (OFF) to toggle BYD's secret ADAS debugging mode on the cluster. |
| **0.3.1-alpha** | 67 | **Optimisation: Elegant App Killer** — Replaced the blind Binder sequence (service call 23/24) with a custom headless Java process (TaskRemover). DashCast now uses Android's native reflection inside an ADB shell to gracefully and accurately find the correct removeTask function regardless of the DiLink version or OEM tweaks. |
| **0.3.0-alpha** | 66 | **Feature: Per-App Resizing!** — You can now save individual Horizontal & Vertical inset values for each application separately using quick-access sliders directly accessible under the Mirror preview panel in DashCast, while the target app is running! (No more global resizing conflicts between Media and Navigation apps) |
| **0.2.95-alpha** | 65 | **Hotfix 5: Ghost Apps in Recents (Android 10/DiLink 3.0)** — Fixed the regex matching for ADB task removal. DashCast now successfully identifies and removes the active Task ID from the App Manager (Recents) so DiLink no longer mistakenly considers the app as running in the background after clicking the Red Cross. |
| **0.2.94-alpha** | 64 | **Hotfix 4: Kill Button Behavior** — Fixed an issue where terminating the active app with the red cross also stopped the ClusterService projection engine. The VirtualDisplay is now left intact when killing an app, allowing the user to seamlessly launch another app to the dashboard without needing to re-activate the engine. Note: Original fix failed injection sync. |
| **0.2.93-alpha** | 63 | **Hotfix 3: Kill Button Behavior** — Fixed an issue where terminating the active app with the red cross also stopped the ClusterService projection engine. The VirtualDisplay is now left intact when killing an app, allowing the user to seamlessly launch another app to the dashboard without needing to re-activate the engine. |
| **0.2.92-alpha** | 62 | **Hotfix 2: Android 10 am task compatibility** — Further fallbacks added to delete the ghost tasks in Android UI. Attempts `am task rm`, `am stack remove` or a low-level Binder call `service call activity 23` depending on ROM customizations. Warnings suppressed to allow the final `am force-stop` to execute cleanly. |
| **0.2.91-alpha** | 61 | **Hotfix: Android 10 am task compatibility** — Replaced `am task rm` with the formally valid `am task remove` command to ensure the Recents UI ghostly task is successfully deleted before `am force-stop`. |
| **0.2.9-alpha** | 60 | **Process & Task Killer** — Fixed an issue where terminating an app with the red cross only killed its background processes but left it in the 'Recent Apps' overview. ADB now identifies `TaskRecord` IDs and executes `am task rm` to fully clear them from Recents UI before issuing `force-stop`. |
| **0.2.8-alpha** | 59 | **Interactive OTA UI** — Enhanced the Over-The-Air updater. Users are now presented with a dialog displaying the full changelog natively fetched from GitHub releases. Added explicit 'Update Now' vs 'Update Later' choices rather than forced auto-download. |
| **0.2.7-alpha** | 58 | **Auto-Launch Checkbox Fix** — Restored visibility and binding logic for the 'Auto' checkbox in the list/grid views. |
